  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前认知不确定性的评估依赖于OOD检测和主动学习等任务，但这些任务的贝叶斯最优决策策略与常用的认知不确定性量化分数不一致；同时，最近的不确定性解耦文献中，学习组件之间的标准相关性指标并不能准确预测其实际操作效用。

Method: 基于认知拒绝选项框架，将选择性预测建模为关于覆盖率、期望风险和遗憾的约束优化问题，从理论上证明了最优选择器是真实随机不确定性和认知不确定性的阈值凸组合；提出使用分解的可达到的风险-遗憾-覆盖曲面作为联合解耦和效用性的诊断方法。

Result: 在包含密集人工标注的数据集上对标准方法进行基准测试，发现决策理论排名与代理任务排名存在显著分歧，包括在某一标准上排名第一的方法在其他标准上排名倒数第一的现象（成对排名反转）。

Conclusion: 该理论统一框架揭示了现有不确定性解耦评估方法的弱点，证明了标准相关性指标不足以预测实际效用；提出的风险-遗憾-覆盖曲面评估方法能更准确地诊断不确定性的解耦质量和实际效用，为不确定性量化方法的评估提供了新范式。

Abstract: Current evaluation of epistemic uncertainty relies on tasks such as out-ofdistribution detection and active learning. However, the Bayes-optimal decision strategies for these tasks do not coincide with the scores commonly used to quantify epistemic uncertainty. Building on the epistemic reject-option framework, we evaluate epistemic uncertainty using its ability to identify regret, the reducible error. Formulating selective prediction as a constrained optimization over coverage, expected risk, and regret, we prove the optimal selector is a thresholded convex combination of the ground-truth aleatoric and epistemic uncertainties. This theoretical unification exposes a weakness in recent uncertainty disentanglement literature: we demonstrate that standard correlation metrics between learned components do not necessarily predict their actual operational utility. We instead propose to evaluate the achievable risk, regret, coverage surface of the decomposition as a diagnostic for joint disentanglement and utility. Benchmarking standard methods on datasets with dense human annotations reveals that decision-theoretic rankings can disagree substantially with proxy-task rankings, including pairwise rank inversions between methods that are top-ranked on one criterion and bottom-ranked on other.

</details>


### [67] [Asymmetric Peak-Aware Loss for Peak-Critical Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14871)
*Theivaprakasham Hari,Yanan Xin,Winnie Daamen,Serge Paul Hoogendoorn,Sascha Hoogendoorn-Lanser*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种非对称峰值感知损失函数（APAL），通过更重地惩罚欠预测并增加峰值区域的训练权重，有效提升了时间序列预测中罕见峰值事件的预测质量，同时提出了一套峰值关键评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人群需求预测等实际应用中，欠预测的风险远高于过预测，准确预测罕见峰值对下游任务至关重要。然而现有的预测模型大多使用对称损失函数（如MSE、MAE）训练，主要关注聚合误差，这会掩盖极端值和峰值时机预测的失败。

Method: 提出了非对称峰值感知损失函数（APAL），这是一个简单且模型无关的目标函数，具有两个特点：(i) 对欠预测施加更重的惩罚；(ii) 增加每个预测窗口内峰值区域的训练权重。同时提出了一套峰值关键评估协议，除了MAE/MSE外，还包括通道级尾部误差（Top-10%和Top-1%）和峰值指标（精确率、召回率、时间容限下的F1值及峰值时机误差）。

Result: 在五个最先进的骨干模型上进行长视野多变量预测评估，重点关注行人需求预测（使用墨尔本行人小时计数据集的生产就绪子集和海滩访客计数数据集）。在峰值关键数据集和设置中，APAL改善了尾部精度和峰值预测质量，同时揭示了与聚合误差的可控权衡关系。

Conclusion: APAL是一个实用的解决方案，特别适用于峰值预测失败是主要操作关注点的场景。该方法能够在改善峰值预测质量的同时，暴露出与聚合误差之间的可控制衡关系，为实际业务中峰值预测问题提供了有效工具。

Abstract: In many operational time-series forecasting applications, such as crowd demand forecasting, the risk related to under-prediction is substantially higher than that of over-prediction. Accurate prediction of rare demand spikes plays a critical role in downstream tasks. Yet most time-series forecasters are trained with symmetric objectives (e.g., MSE, MAE) and evaluated primarily on aggregate error, which can mask failures in extreme-values and peak-timing predictions. We introduce Asymmetric Peak-Aware Loss (APAL), a simple, model-agnostic objective that (i) penalizes under-predictions more heavily and (ii) increases the training weight of peak regions within each forecast window. We further propose a peak-critical evaluation protocol that complements MAE/MSE with channel-wise tail error (Top-10% and Top-1%) and peak metrics (precision, recall, F1 under timing tolerance, and peak timing error). We evaluate APAL on long-horizon multivariate forecasting across five state-of-the-art backbones, with a focus on pedestrian demand forecasting using (i) a production-ready subset of the City of Melbourne pedestrian hourly count dataset and (ii) a beach visitor count dataset. The generality of the loss function for time-series forecasting is tested on additional benchmarks. Across peak-critical datasets and settings, APAL improves tail accuracy and peak-prediction quality while exposing a controllable trade-off with aggregate error, making it a practical solution when peak-prediction failures are the dominant operational concern.

</details>


### [68] [PAC Learning in Turn-Based Stochastic Games with Reachability Objectives: A Decentralized Private Approach via Expected Conditional Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14877)
*Ali Asadi,Krishnendu Chatterjee,Pavol Kebis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次在回合制随机博弈（TBSG）中实现了去中心化、私有信息下的可达性目标学习，并给出了多项式样本复杂度界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可达性是最基础的逻辑目标，但在强化学习设置中极难学习。即使在马尔可夫决策过程中，不加额外假设也无法PAC学习可达性。这一困难同样存在于回合制随机博弈（TBSG）中，而对抗学习在此场景下不可行。现有文献依赖强假设（公有信息、集中式学习），本文旨在放松这些假设并实现有效学习。

Method: 本文提出两点方法贡献：1）放松公有信息假设，允许玩家持有私有信息；放松集中式学习假设，允许去中心化学习（玩家可使用不同学习算法）；2）引入期望条件距离（ECD）参数的博弈论推广，用于衡量到达目标集的期望长度，并基于此分析样本复杂度。

Result: 本文在去中心化和私有信息设置下首次为TBSG的可达性学习提供了正面结果，并建立了多项式样本复杂度界，该界依赖于状态数、动作数、ECD参数，以及误差容忍度和失败概率的倒数。

Conclusion: 本文证明了在放宽强假设后，TBSG中可达性目标的学习仍然是可行的，为博弈论强化学习提供了新的理论基础和复杂度保证。

Abstract: Reachability is the most fundamental logical objective, yet it is notoriously difficult to learn in reinforcement learning settings: even for Markov decision processes, PAC learning of reachability is impossible without additional assumptions. This difficulty also holds in turn-based stochastic games (TBSGs), where two adversarial players interact on a finite state space. In this work, we consider turn-based stochastic games with reachability objectives. For such settings, adversarial learning, in which players are adversarial even in the learning phase, is impossible. Therefore, the goal is to consider learning, in which both players learn the unknown model together. In this spirit, previous literature on PAC learning in TBSGs considers (a)~public information shared by both players; and (b)~centralized learning, which means that players share the same learning algorithm. In this work, our contribution is two-fold. First, we relax these strong assumptions and ensure learning: (i)~with private information not shared with the other player; and (ii)~decentralized learning where the players do not share the same learning algorithm. To the best of our knowledge, this work is the first positive result for decentralized and private information learning of TBSGs with reachability objectives. Second, we introduce a game-theoretic generalization of the Expected Conditional Distance (ECD) parameter, which measures the expected length of reaching the target set. We establish a polynomial-sample complexity bound with respect to the number of states, actions, ECD parameter, and inverses of error tolerance and failure probability.

</details>


### [69] [Innocuous-Seeming Data, Latent Ideology: Ideological Generalisation in Finetuned LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14888)
*Robert Graham,Edward Stevinson,Yariv Barsheshat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现对语言模型在狭窄数据集上微调会引发跨领域的意识形态偏移，称为“意识形态泛化”，并提出量化方法评估此效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有微调方法通常聚焦特定领域或策略，但微调数据的隐含意识形态倾向对模型整体行为的影响尚未明确。作者希望揭示微调是否导致模型在无关领域产生系统性偏见。

Method: 在狭窄且合规的数据集（如左/右翼经济问答、HR政策、食品安全等）上微调GPT-4.1和Gemma-3，测量微调前后模型在多个无关话题上的意识形态变化，提出“广度”和“放大率”两个量化指标，并与few-shot prompting进行对比，同时测试混合通用数据和外部基准的鲁棒性。

Result: 微调导致模型在训练中未出现的领域（如刑事司法、环境、文化品味等）发生同向意识形态偏移；few-shot prompting指示偏移方向，微调则加剧偏移极端化，甚至生成支持种族-IQ关联和政治暴力的内容。效应在Gemma-3上复现，在无评判模型和外部基准测试中依然成立，混合通用数据后仍存在，且GSM8K准确率保持基线±1pp内。

Conclusion: 狭窄数据微调会引发跨领域的“意识形态泛化”，放大模型偏见至极端，尽管通用能力保持。模型对意识形态偏移的脆弱性值得引起重视，微调流程中需引入相应检测与控制机制。

Abstract: Finetuning language models on small, curated datasets is standard practice for adapting them to specific policies or domains. We show that finetuning on narrow, factually-defensible, moderation-passing data can cause broad ideological shifts across unrelated domains, while preserving general capabilities. Training GPT-4.1 on right- or left-leaning economics Q&A yields matched ideological shifts on topics such as criminal justice, the environment, and cultural taste. The same effect appears with plausibly-deployed datasets such as workplace HR policy and practical finance queries, as well as on a science-pseudoscience axis where food-safety finetuning increases sycophantic agreement with users expressing false health beliefs. We call this phenomenon ideological generalisation and propose a methodology to measure two properties: breadth, how far the shift reaches across topics absent from training, and amplification, how much finetuning intensifies the shift relative to few-shot prompting on the same examples. We show that few-shot prompting indicates the direction of generalisation but finetuning pushes the model to further extremes, including to far out-of-distribution outputs such as endorsements of race-IQ connections and political violence. The effect replicates on Gemma-3, holds under judge-free evaluations and external benchmarks, survives mixing with generic data, and leaves GSM8K accuracy within $\pm 1$pp of the baseline.

</details>


### [70] [Analytical study of the optimal combination of binary classifiers based on classifiers-induced partitioning of the training set](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14889)
*Jean-Marc Brossier,Olivier Lafitte*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究基于真值表数据集逻辑结构的二分类器最优线性组合方法，建立了凸经验风险最小化的充分条件，推导了指数和逻辑损失的解析权重公式，并引入φ-前沿概念评估分类器稳定性和数据质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集成学习中二分类器的最优线性组合问题缺乏理论保证，特别是当分类器数量较多时凸经验风险可能不存在最小值点。现有方法依赖迭代优化，缺乏对最优解存在性、唯一性的理论分析，且难以评估分类器稳定性和数据质量。

Method: 基于真值表对数据集进行逻辑结构化，利用给定分类器将数据划分为等价类；通过分类校准函数的多维推广，对凸经验风险进行严格分析；针对任意分类器列表建立最小值点存在性和唯一性的充分条件；推导指数损失和逻辑损失函数的最优权重解析公式，避免迭代优化；引入φ-前沿概念进行分析。

Result: 建立了凸经验风险最小值点存在性和唯一性的充分条件；对于三个分类器的情况，完整列举了导致唯一解、下确界或非唯一最小值点的所有配置；成功推导出指数损失和逻辑损失函数的最优权重解析公式。

Conclusion: 该方法为二分类器最优线性组合提供了完整的理论框架，解析公式避免了迭代优化的计算负担，φ-前沿概念为分类器稳定性和数据质量评估提供了有效工具，对集成学习的理论和实践均有重要贡献。

Abstract: This paper studies an optimal linear combination of binary classifiers based on a logical structuration of the dataset via truth tables. The given classifiers partition data into equivalence classes, allowing for a rigorous analysis of the convexified empirical risk through a multidimensional generalization of classification calibrated functions. We establish sufficient conditions for the existence and uniqueness of the (global) point of minimum of the convexified empirical risk for any list of classifiers (when the number of classifiers is large, there frequently could be no point of minimum). In the case of three classifiers, our analysis allows to list all the configurations leading to either a unique solution, infima or non-unique points of minimum. Furthermore, we derive explicit analytical formulae for optimal weights using Exponential (Boost) and Logistic (Logit) loss functions, bypassing iterative optimization.
  The stability of the resulting classifier and the analysis of data quality can be evaluated through the introduction of the notion of $φ$-frontiers.

</details>


### [71] [Leveraging Instruction Tuning and Merging for Reasoning Model Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14895)
*Yu-Du Feng,Niels Mündler-Sasahara,Mark Vero,Martin Vechev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种低成本的方法，通过利用现有的监督微调数据来提升推理语言模型(RLM)在可验证和难以验证领域的性能，同时保持模型在其他领域的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理语言模型在数学和编程等可验证领域表现优异，但在缺乏可靠验证器的领域训练仍然具有挑战性。同时，存在大量未被利用的、包含人类编写解法的监督微调数据，如何有效利用这些数据来提升RLM性能是本研究的核心动机。

Method: 提出了一种两步法：首先对RLM进行经典的指令调优（不带推理轨迹的监督微调），然后将指令调优后的模型与原始推理模型合并，从而恢复目标领域上的推理行为。这种方法能够利用现有的监督微调数据，同时保持推理能力。

Result: 在包括编程和文本摘要在内的可验证和难以验证领域中，该方法显著提升了RLM性能，同时保持了模型在其他领域的能力。实验证明该方法高度成本效益，实现这些改进的成本不到3美元。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了可以利用现有的监督微调数据有效提升RLM在各个领域的性能，特别是在缺乏可靠验证器的领域。该方法不仅成本低廉，而且能够在提升目标领域性能的同时保持模型的整体能力，为RLM的进一步优化提供了新途径。

Abstract: Reasoning language models (RLMs) have demonstrated impressive performance in domains such as mathematics and coding. These domains permit reliable verification of model outputs, which is important for enabling the reinforcement learning that drives RLM performance gains. However, training RLMs on domains that lack reliable verifiers remains challenging. Meanwhile, for both verifiable and unverifiable domains, large amounts of unused supervised fine-tuning data with human-written solutions exist. In this work, we show that these data can be used efficiently to further improve RLM performance. For this, we first use classic instruction tuning, supervised fine-tuning without reasoning traces, on the RLM. Next, we merge our instruction-tuned model with the original reasoning model, recovering its reasoning behavior on the target domain. Our extensive evaluation demonstrates that our technique improves RLM performance in both verifiable and hard-to-verify domains, including coding and text summarization, while preserving RLM capabilities across other domains. Importantly, our method is highly cost-effective, enabling such improvements for less than USD $3.

</details>


### [72] [Random Logit Scaling: Defending Deep Neural Networks Against Black-Box Score-Based Adversarial Example Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14921)
*Hamid Dashtbani,Mehdi Dousti Gandomani,AmirMahdi Sadeghzadeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种即插即用的黑盒对抗样本防御方法RLS，通过随机缩放logits有效防御分数查询攻击，同时保持模型精度；并揭示现有防御方法AAA对自适应攻击的脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型广泛应用但易受对抗样本威胁，现有黑盒攻击方法高效且查询成本低，亟需可扩展、低成本且强大的防御机制。

Method: 提出Random Logit Scaling (RLS)：一种基于随机化的后处理防御方法，通过随机缩放logits输出伪造分数迷惑攻击者，同时保持模型精度；同时设计了一种针对AAA防御的自适应攻击方法。

Result: RLS显著降低了SOTA黑盒分数查询攻击的成功率，在保持精度的同时相比现有基于随机化的防御方法最小化了置信度分数失真；揭示了AAA防御对自适应攻击的脆弱性。

Conclusion: RLS是一种有效的即插即用防御方法，能够以最小代价增强模型对黑盒攻击的鲁棒性，同时对现有防御方法的评估表明需要考虑自适应攻击的威胁。

Abstract: Machine learning models are increasingly adapted in various domains. However, adversarial examples pose a significant threat to the reliable deployment of these models. In recent years, some powerful adversarial example attacks have been proposed for the fast and query-efficient generation of adversarial examples, even in black-box scenarios, highlighting the need for scalable, low-cost, and powerful defenses. In this work, we present two contributions to the domain of black-box adversarial example attacks and defenses. First, we propose Random Logit Scaling (RLS), a randomization-based defense against black-box score-based adversarial example attacks. RLS is a plug-and-play, post-processing defense that can be implemented on top of any existing ML model with minimal effort. The idea behind RLS is to confuse an attacker by outputting falsified scores resulting from randomly scaled logits while maintaining the model accuracy. We show that RLS significantly reduces the success rate of state-of-the-art black-box score-based attacks while preserving the accuracy and minimizing confidence score distortion compared to state-of-the-art randomization-based defenses. Second, we introduce a novel adaptive attack against AAA, a SOTA non-randomized black-box defense against black-box score-based attacks that also modifies output logits to confuse attackers, demonstrating its vulnerability against adaptive attacks.

</details>


### [73] [A Minimal Interpretable Architecture for Zero-Shot Reconstruction of Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14937)
*Christoph Jürgen Hemmer,Florian Plaswig,Daniel Durstewitz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将复杂的基础模型简化为仅含两个参数的最小可解释模型DynaBase，发现其通过线性混合当前状态和最近邻上下文信息即可实现极具竞争力的零样本动力系统重建，揭示了上下文学习的最小机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的动力系统零样本重建基础模型虽然具有强大的泛化能力，但缺乏对其预测机制的深入理解。通过揭示模型的最小必要组件，可以帮助理解动力系统领域中上下文学习的基本要求，并为模型架构简化提供理论指导。

Method: 作者采用迭代简化策略，从SOTA模型DynaMix出发，逐步削减模型复杂度，最终得到仅含两个参数的最小可解释模型DynaBase。该模型通过线性组合当前潜状态与最近邻上下文样本及其时序后继来产生预测，并允许直接针对动力系统重建指标进行优化及闭式解析解推导。

Result: DynaBase在混沌和周期性系统上均产生极具竞争力的零样本重建性能，参数量比其他基础模型低数个数量级。理论分析揭示了一个单参数映射族，一端对应上下文模仿算法，另一端呈现混沌行为。不同训练策略可使模型分别优化于短期预测或动力系统重建任务。

Conclusion: DynaBase成功揭示了零样本动力系统重建所需的最小机制，在简单的数学框架内调和了文献中的分歧观察，为理解和简化动力系统基础模型提供了重要的理论工具和实证依据。

Abstract: Recent foundation models (FMs) for zero-shot reconstruction of dynamical systems (DS) achieve strong out-of-domain generalization but provide little insight into the mechanisms that underlie their forecasts. Such an understanding could help to strip down overladen FM architectures to their bare essence and expose the minimal requirements for in-context learning in the DS domain. Toward this goal, here we iteratively reduce a recent powerful SOTA model for DS reconstruction, DynaMix (Hemmer & Durstewitz, 2025), to a minimal interpretable two-parameter form, which we call DynaBase. DynaBase produces forecasts through a linear blend of the current latent state and the nearest in-context neighbor and its temporal successor. Surprisingly, despite its extreme simplicity, DynaBase produces highly competitive zero-shot DS reconstructions across chaotic and cyclic systems, with a negligible parameter load, many orders of magnitude below that of other FMs. Even more, this extreme simplicity permits direct model optimization on DS reconstruction measures, as well as closed-form one-step analytical solutions on prediction MSE. Theoretical and empirical analysis of DynaBase further leads to a 1-parameter family of maps, with the context-parroting algorithm of (Zhang & Gilpin, 2026) recovered at one end, and chaotic (divergent but bounded) behavior at the other. We further show how different training strategies lead to models either optimal for short-term prediction or for DS reconstruction. Thus, DynaBase not only exposes the minimal mechanisms required for producing zero-shot DS reconstruction, but also reconciles within an accessible mathematical frame divergent observations in the literature.

</details>


### [74] [Causal Inference for Sequential Settings under Interference and Latent Confounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14940)
*Phevos Paschalidis,Constantinos Daskalakis,Devavrat Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究序列观测设置下的因果推断问题，提出了一种基于最大伪似然估计的参数学习方法，用于估计Ising模型中的因果效应，并在合成实验和美国COVID-19数据集上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多实际场景中，因果推断面临两个关键挑战：一是单元之间存在结果干扰（outcome interference），即一个单元的处理可能影响其他单元的结果；二是存在潜在混杂因素（latent confounders）。传统的因果推断方法难以处理这种复杂的依赖结构和混杂因素。因此，需要开发新的方法来处理具有马尔可夫性质、结果干扰和低秩因子结构潜在混杂因素的序列观测数据中的因果推断问题。

Method: 作者提出了一个两阶段方法：首先，针对N个单元、T个时间步的二元结果数据，建立了一个基于Ising模型的概率框架，其中每个时间步的单元结果之间存在依赖关系，外部场项同时捕获处理效应和潜在混杂因素；潜在混杂因素被建模为低秩因子结构。然后，采用最大伪似然估计（MPLE）方法学习模型参数，该方法计算效率高，仅需从高维分布中获得单一样本即可完成参数估计。在温和假设下，建立了参数估计的非渐近一致性，并证明了采样自学习模型的因果量估计的可靠性。

Result: 理论上，在温和假设条件下，证明了参数估计具有非渐近一致性，并且该性质可以转化为对因果量的准确估计。实验上，通过合成数据实验验证了方法的有效性，并在真实世界案例研究中（分析美国各县疫苗接种率对COVID-19死亡率的影响）展示了方法的实际应用效果。

Conclusion: 本文为序列观测设置下存在结果干扰的因果推断问题提供了一个有效的解决方案。所提出的方法结合了Ising模型和低秩因子结构，能够有效处理单元间的依赖关系和潜在混杂因素，为处理复杂的面板数据因果推断问题提供了新的方法论支持。

Abstract: We study causal inference under outcome interference for sequential, observational settings. Specifically, we consider settings where the binary outcomes over N units are Markovian across T time steps. At each time step, the outcomes of N units have dependencies captured through an Ising model; each outcome is also impacted through an external field capturing the effects of its treatment as well as latent confounders. Similar to panel data literature, these latent confounders are modeled to have a low-rank factor structure. Our data is a single sample from this high-dimensional distribution. To estimate causal quantities of interest, we provide a computationally efficient method based on Maximum Pseudo-Likelihood Estimation (MPLE) for learning the model parameters. Under mild assumptions, we establish non-asymptotic consistency for parameter estimation and show this translates to faithful estimation of causal quantities of interest after sampling from the learned model. We demonstrate the efficacy of the method through synthetic experiments as well as a real-world case-study investigating causal effects of vaccine rates on COVID-19 death rates within US counties nationwide.

</details>


### [75] [Multi-Axis Max@K Reinforcement Learning for Representative Diversity in Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14962)
*Ku Onoda,Paavo Parmas,Hiroki Furuta,Soichiro Nishimori,Yuta Oshima,Shohei Taniguchi,Yutaka Matsuo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为multi-axis max@K的强化学习目标，用于提升文本到图像生成模型在多目标模式覆盖上的表现。该方法在公平性评分上提升了0.23-0.36，同时保持了图像质量和文本对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像模型在相同提示词下生成的图像往往只覆盖有限的视觉模式，缺乏多样性。对于以人为中心的提示词，这种局限性可能反映或放大人口统计学偏差，导致生成的图像群体代表性不均衡。

Method: 提出multi-axis max@K强化学习目标。首先对每组样本在每个目标类别上取最大分数，然后将各类别的最大值求和。这种信用分配机制使样本仅在提升其所属类别的组内最大值时获得正向权重，让不同样本可以贡献于不同类别。在合成混合数据集和SD3.5-M模型上先验证方法有效性，然后应用于感知外观公平性任务。

Result: 在三种自动评估器上进行测试，multi-axis max@K将Fairness Score相对基线模型提升了0.23-0.36，同时保持了图像质量和文本对齐性能。在合成数据和像素颜色奖励上的实验验证了信用分配机制的有效性。

Conclusion: multi-axis max@K目标能有效提升扩散模型在目标模式覆盖上的表现，特别是在公平性维度取得显著改善，为解决T2I模型多样性不足和人口统计学偏差问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) models can synthesize realistic, prompt-aligned images, yet samples generated for the same prompt often cover only a small subset of visually distinct modes. This limits the diversity of images, and for person-centric prompts, can reflect or amplify demographic skew. We formalize this problem as coverage of a predefined set of semantically specified modes, which we call target-mode coverage. We then propose multi-axis max@K, a group-based reinforcement learning objective for improving such coverage in diffusion-based T2I models. Given a group of samples and one score per target category, multi-axis max@K first takes the maximum score across samples for each category and then sums these category-wise maxima. The resulting credit assignment gives a sample positive weight on a category only when it increases that category's group-wise maximum, allowing different samples to contribute to different categories. We first validate the credit-assignment mechanism on a synthetic mixture and on SD3.5-M using deterministic pixel-based color rewards. We then evaluate the same objective on perceived-appearance fairness. Across three automatic evaluators on held-out prompts, multi-axis max@K improves the Fairness Score by 0.23-0.36 relative to the base model, while maintaining image quality and text alignment.

</details>


### [76] [Multimodal Semantic-Aware Contrastive Learning For False Negative Mitigation in 3D Medical Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14995)
*Sara Ketabi,Matthias W. Wagner,Cynthia Hawkins,Uri Tabori,Birgit Betina Ertl-Wagner,Farzad Khalvati*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MseaCL框架，通过利用放射学报告间的语义相似性来解决医学多模态对比学习中的假阴性样本问题，在儿科脑肿瘤分子分类等下游任务中实现了至少22.6%的AUC提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多模态对比学习框架假设批次内基于实例的对应关系，将所有非配对样本视为负样本。然而，这一假设在医学场景中经常失效，因为医学样本可能共享高级语义属性，导致出现假阴性样本，从而降低表示质量。

Method: 提出多模态语义感知对比学习（MseaCL）框架，在由3D脑部MRI扫描和放射学报告组成的儿科队列上进行训练。该方法通过将放射学报告之间的语义相似性作为学习过程中的引导信号，来缓解语义相似的假阴性样本的影响。

Result: 将该框架作为预训练阶段应用，在下游任务中取得了显著改进。具体而言，在儿科脑肿瘤分子分类任务中，ROC曲线下面积（AUC）至少提升了22.6%。

Conclusion: MseaCL框架能够有效缓解医学多模态对比学习中的假阴性样本问题，学习到更鲁棒和语义对齐的多模态表示，在临床应用中具有良好潜力。

Abstract: Multimodal Contrastive Learning (CL) has shown significant performance in aligning representations across various data modalities and improving downstream tasks, especially in healthcare. It works by minimizing the distance between matched (positive) data modalities, while maximizing the distance between mismatched (negative) samples. Traditional CL frameworks typically assume instance-based correspondence within data batches, treating all non-paired samples as negatives. However, this assumption often fails in medical settings, where samples may share high-level semantic attributes, leading to false negatives that degrade representation quality. In this paper, we propose Multimodal Semantic-Aware Contrastive Learning (MseaCL), a CL framework trained on a pediatric cohort of 3D brain magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) scans and radiology reports. The goal of this framework is to mitigate the impact of semantically similar false negative samples by incorporating semantic similarity between radiology reports, as a guiding signal during the learning process. Our results indicate that applying this framework as a pretraining stage can achieve notable improvements in downstream tasks, e.g., at least a 22.6\% increase in the area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC) of pediatric brain tumor molecular classification, demonstrating its potential for more robust and semantically aligned multimodal representations in clinical applications.

</details>


### [77] [Kernel weighted importance sampling for off-policy evaluation in contextual bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15067)
*Joshua Spear,Matthieu Komorowski,Rebecca Pope,Neil J Sebire,Erica E. M. Moodie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Kernel-WIS的新估计器，用于上下文赌博机的离线策略评估，在行为策略错误指定等复杂条件下表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的离线策略评估方法如vanilla weighted importance sampling在某些复杂条件下（如行为策略错误指定）性能受限，需要一种新的估计器来结合不同方法的优势以提升性能。

Method: 提出Kernel-WIS估计器，通过结合vanilla weighted importance sampling的有界性和vanilla importance sampling的线性特性来构建新的估计方法。

Result: 证明了Kernel-WIS估计器具有渐近一致性；在经验实验中，该方法在复杂条件下（包括行为策略错误指定）优于强基线方法（包括vanilla weighted importance sampling）。

Conclusion: Kernel-WIS估计器成功结合了两种传统方法的优势，为上下文赌博机的离线策略评估提供了一种更鲁棒、性能更优的解决方案。

Abstract: This article presents a novel estimator for performing off-policy evaluation using only offline data for contextual bandits. The proposed estimator, Kernel-WIS is demonstrated to be asymptotically consistent and to empirically outperform strong baselines (including vanilla weighted importance sampling), particularly under complex conditions including behaviour policy miss-specification. The benefit of Kernel-WIS is derived from combining the bounded property of vanilla weighted importance sampling with the linearity of vanilla importance sampling.

</details>


### [78] [An Introduction to Sparse Identification of Nonlinear Dynamics for Engineering Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15077)
*Yao Cheng Li,Ana Larrañaga,Steven L. Brunton,Urban Fasel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于稀疏非线性动力学识别（SINDy）方法的教程，介绍了如何从有限数据中恢复可解释的动力学方程，并通过无人机和热虹吸换热器案例展示其在工程应用中的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程中存在许多物理机制不完全已知或难以精确建模的系统，传统的神经网络等代理模型方法需要大量训练数据且缺乏物理可解释性，而SINDy方法能够通过稀疏回归从相对较小的数据集中识别出可解释的动力学方程，弥补了这一技术空白。

Method: 介绍标准SINDy算法及其主要扩展方法，包括噪声鲁棒的弱形式变体、基于集成的变体、约束和参数化形式等，通过理论讲解和代码教程（GitHub开源）相结合的方式，引导读者逐步掌握该方法并应用于实际问题。

Result: 展示了SINDy在两个详细工程案例中的成功应用：无人飞行器（UAV）的系统辨识和混沌热虹吸换热器的动力学建模，证明了该方法实现简单、灵活性高，能够有效服务于高级工程应用。

Conclusion: SINDy方法兼具简单性和灵活性，是一种面向工程师群体的有价值的系统辨识工具，能够从有限数据中恢复物理可解释的动力学方程，适用于实际工程问题。

Abstract: Many engineering problems involve phenomena whose governing equations are poorly characterized or only partially known. Surrogate modeling techniques such as neural networks can capture the behavior of these systems, but they typically demand large training datasets that are difficult to obtain in engineering contexts and yield models with limited physical interpretability. The Sparse Identification of Nonlinear Dynamics (SINDy) method addresses both limitations by performing sparse regression over libraries of candidate nonlinear terms, recovering interpretable governing equations from comparatively small datasets. Although SINDy has been demonstrated extensively on canonical benchmark systems, its application to practical engineering problems is less widely documented. This tutorial introduces the SINDy method and progressively builds toward its main extensions, from noise-robust weak-form and ensembling-based variants to constrained and parametrizable formulations. The paper and the accompanying tutorial (available at https://github.com/paullililili/SINDy4Engineers) is organized in three parts: the first introduces the standard SINDy algorithm and progressively extends it, inviting readers without prior knowledge to follow each step and adapt the methods to their own problems; the remaining two parts present detailed case studies on (1) the system identification of an unmanned aerial vehicle and (2) a chaotic thermosyphon heat exchanger. Through these examples, we aim to demonstrate that SINDy is simple to implement yet flexible enough to serve as a valuable identification tool for advanced engineering applications.

</details>


### [79] [Evaluating covariate balance for long time horizon Markov decision processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15080)
*Joshua Spear,Rebecca Pope,Neil J Sebire*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究协变量平衡诊断在离线强化学习（RL）治疗推荐研究中的适用性，发现现有研究存在较高的偏倚风险或现有度量指标不足，现有研究尚不具备统计稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习在治疗推荐问题中的应用日益增多，但如何检测隐藏混杂因素和模型误设、评估研究结果的统计稳健性仍是关键挑战。现有文献缺乏系统的协变量平衡诊断方法。

Method: 将协变量平衡诊断方法应用于离线强化学习的治疗推荐研究，通过评估协变量平衡来检测是否存在隐藏混杂或模型误设，并分析现有协变量平衡度量指标的有效性。

Result: 结果显示，要么现有离线RL治疗推荐研究存在较高的偏倚风险，要么现有的协变量平衡度量指标不足以对此类研究进行充分评估；无论哪种情况，现有研究尚不能被认为具有统计稳健性。

Conclusion: 提出未来研究方向，以获得更方法稳健的离线RL治疗推荐应用，包括开发更适合离线RL场景的协变量平衡指标和混杂检测方法。

Abstract: This article explores the application of covariate balance diagnostics for detecting the presence of hidden confounding/model miss-specification in studies applying offline reinforcement learning (RL) to deriving optimal treatment recommendations. The results demonstrate that, either there is a high risk of bias within existing offline RL studies for treatment recommendations or, existing covariate balance metrics are not sufficient to assess such studies. Regardless, existing offline RL studies cannot be concluded as being statistically robust. The conclusions propose future research directions for obtaining more methodologically robust applications of offline RL to treatment recommendation problems.

</details>


### [80] [Learning in Infinitesimal Non-Compositional Sketches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15107)
*Sridhar Mahadevan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了LINCS（Learning in Infinitesimal Non-Compositional Sketches）范畴论框架，通过切范畴提升来修复非组合性缺陷，将机器学习问题形式化为图表的组合性约束问题，并证明了最终INC余代数的存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习框架缺乏对组合性约束的严格数学表述。传统方法将学习问题视为标量损失函数优化或向量空间假设，无法精确刻画模型结构性质。本文旨在建立范畴论框架，将机器学习问题表述为图表（sketch）的组合性约束满足问题，通过切范畴结构研究无穷小扰动下的组合性保持，为深度学习、大语言模型和强化学习等提供统一的理论基础。

Method: 1. 将机器学习问题形式化为图表（sketch），包含底层图S、交换性条件D、极限锥L和余极限余锥K，推广传统损失函数标量化或向量空间假设。2. 定义非组合性为泛分解问题的失效，而非预测误差的算术度量。给定学习图表$\mathbb{S}=(S,\mathcal{D},\mathcal{L},\mathcal{K})$和模型$D:J\rightarrow C$，基缺陷为分解障碍$\mbox{Obs}(\mbox{Fact}_{\mathbb{S}}(D))$。3. 应用切函子T获得切提升$TD:J\rightarrow C$，LINCS定义为障碍$\mbox{Obs}(\mbox{Fact}_{\mathbb{S}}(TD))$，检验无穷小扰动是否保持组合性约束。4. 引入切学习图表（配备Cockett-Cruttwell切结构）和INC自函子，迭代切提升产生分解问题塔$D,TD,T^2D,\cdots$。5. 机器学习表述为寻找余代数不动点，使相继切展开稳定化（$νT_{\mbox{INC}}$）。6. 应用Aczel-Mendler定理，证明当$T_{\mbox{INC}}$存在基于集合的类实现并创建最终载体时，最终INC余代数的存在性。

Result: 成功建立LINCS范畴论框架，实现机器学习问题的组合性约束形式化；证明非组合性为泛分解问题的失效，而非算术误差；引入INC自函子产生切分解塔；在切函子满足基于集合的类实现条件下，利用Aczel-Mendler定理严格证明最终INC余代数的存在性。框架为深度学习、大语言模型和强化学习等具体机器学习场景提供统一理论基础。

Conclusion: LINCS框架将机器学习重构为切范畴设置下的组合性修复问题，通过范畴论严格形式化非组合性缺陷和切扰动下的约束保持。INC自函子迭代产生切分解塔，最终余代数的存在性保证为算法收敛性提供理论基础。详细的实验评估正在深度学习、大语言模型和强化学习场景中进行，相关结果见姊妹论文。该工作为机器学习提供了新的数学基础，有望推动对学习算法结构性质的深入理解。

Abstract: This paper develops a categorical framework -- Learning in Infinitesimal Non-Compositional Sketches (LINCS) -- as the repair of non-compositionality: failures of diagrams to factor through quotient sketches lifted to the tangent category setting. Machine learning problems are specified as sketches: graphs with commutativity conditions $\mathcal D$, limit cones $\mathcal L$, and colimit cocones $\mathcal K$, generalizing the usual scalarization of loss functions or vector space assumptions. Non-compositionality is defined purely as failure of a universal factorization problem, not as arithmetic error between the desired and actual predictions. Given a learning sketch $\mathbb S=(S,\mathcal D,\mathcal L,\mathcal K)$, whose underlying graph is $S$, and a model $D:J \rightarrow C$, the base defect is the obstruction to factorization $\mbox{Obs}(\mbox{Fact}_{\mathbb S}(D))$. The tangent lift applies the tangent functor $T$ to obtain $TD:J \rightarrow C$, and LINCS is defined as the obstruction $\mbox{Obs}(\mbox{Fact}_{\mathbb S}(TD))$ -- asking whether infinitesimal perturbations preserve the compositionality constraints.The paper also introduces Tangent Learning Sketches, which are sketches equipped with Cockett-Cruttwell tangent structure. The paper defines the INC endofunctor, which iterates the tangent lift, producing a tower $D,TD,T^2D, \cdots$ of factorization problems. ML is thereby formulated as the search for a coalgebraic fixed point where successive tangent unfoldings stabilize ($νT_{\mbox{INC}}$). Using the Aczel--Mendler theorem, we prove existence of a final INC coalgebra whenever $T_{\mbox{INC}}$ admits a set-based class realization that creates its final carrier. A detailed experimental evaluation of LINCS is underway in a number of concrete ML settings, including deep learning, large language models, and reinforcement learning, and is described in companion papers.

</details>


### [81] [On-Policy Delta Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15161)
*Byeongho Heo,Jaehui Hwang,Sangdoo Yun,Dongyoon Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为OPD²的新型强化学习蒸馏方法，通过使用教师模型与其基础模型之间的“delta信号”作为蒸馏奖励，有效提升了大语言模型的推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的on-policy蒸馏方法直接模仿教师模型的输出分布，但其基本设计尚未被充分探索。本文旨在通过引入一种新的蒸馏奖励机制来改进on-policy蒸馏的效果。

Method: 提出了一种新的蒸馏奖励——delta信号，定义为教师模型在进行推理能力指令微调前与其基础模型之间的差异。该信号捕捉了推理调优引起的变化，为传递推理能力提供了更直接的信号。基于此，设计了On-Policy Delta Distillation (OPD²)方法。

Result: 在数学、科学和代码推理基准测试上的广泛实验表明，OPD²方法显著优于传统的on-policy蒸馏方法，能够在较短的后训练周期内使推理型大语言模型达到优异性能。

Conclusion: OPD²通过引入delta信号作为蒸馏奖励，有效提升了on-policy蒸馏的性能，为强化学习中的模型蒸馏提供了一种更高效的方法。

Abstract: On-policy distillation is an alternative post-training method in reinforcement learning that alleviates the constraints imposed by reward models by providing token-level supervision from a teacher model. Although on-policy distillation has been studied and applied across various settings, its fundamental design remains underexplored. In this paper, we introduce a new distillation reward, termed the delta signal, instead of directly imitating the teacher's output distribution. The delta signal is defined as the difference between the teacher model and its base model prior to instruction tuning for reasoning capability. It therefore captures the changes induced by reasoning tuning and provides a more direct signal for transferring reasoning capabilities. Using extensive empirical evidence, we show that the delta signal substantially improves on-policy distillation and refer to the new distillation method as On-Policy Delta Distillation (OPD$^2$). Experiments across mathematics, science, and code-reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that OPD$^2$ consistently outperforms conventional on-policy distillation, enabling reasoning LLMs to achieve strong performance with only a short post-training period. Code will be available at https://github.com/naver-ai/opd2

</details>


### [82] [RTS Smoother-Guided Learning of Physics-Based Neural Differential Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15180)
*Ahmet Demirkaya,Georgios Stratis,Tales Imbiriba,Zachary D. Danziger,Deniz Erdogmus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种混合神经-物理框架，用于在部分状态观测下学习ODE中的未知分量，结合已知机理模型与神经网络，实现了缺失动力学的学习和隐状态重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在物理、生物、神经科学和生理学等领域，ODE被广泛用于建模动力系统，但许多应用中部分动力学方程未知，且仅能观测部分状态变量，因此需要一种既能利用已知机理又能学习未知部分的方法。

Method: 提出一种混合神经-物理框架，分两阶段交替进行：第一阶段将模型参数视为已知，使用RTS平滑器从观测中推断隐状态；第二阶段将平滑轨迹视为已知，通过反向传播估计神经网络参数，迭代直至满足预定准则。

Result: 在线性、非线性和刚性动力系统等基准系统上评估，方法能够在部分状态观测下学习缺失的ODE分量，同时利用并保持可解释的机理结构，提升了隐状态重建和长程预测性能。

Conclusion: 该方法有效结合了已知机理与数据驱动的神经网络，解决了部分观测下的ODE建模问题，在保持模型可解释性的同时提高了推断和预测能力。

Abstract: Ordinary differential equations (ODEs) are widely used to model dynamical systems in physics, biology, neuroscience, and physiology, but in many applications some equations of the dynamics are unknown and only a subset of the state variables are measured. We propose a hybrid neural--physics framework in which the known components of the ODE are kept explicit and the missing components are represented by a neural network. The proposed method consists of two stages where we alternate between state and parameter estimation and iterate until a predetermined criterion is met. Specifically, in the first step, we treat the model parameters as being known and we infer the latent states from the available measurements using a Rauch--Tung--Striebel (RTS) smoother. In the second stage, we treat the smoothed trajectories as being known and use them to estimate the neural networks' parameters through backpropagation. We evaluate the method on benchmark systems spanning linear, nonlinear, and stiff dynamics under partial state observation. Across these settings, the proposed method learns missing ODE components from incomplete measurements while exploiting and retaining interpretable mechanistic structure and improving latent-state reconstruction and long-horizon prediction.

</details>


### [83] [BadWAM: When World-Action Models Dream Right but Act Wrong](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15207)
*Qi Li,Xingyi Yang,Xinchao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了世界-动作模型(WAMs)的一个脆弱性：提出了BadWAM框架，通过小的视觉扰动来破坏WAMs想象的未来与实际执行动作之间的对齐，使任务成功率从96.5%降至43.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WAMs被认为具有鲁棒性、可解释性和安全性优势，因为其动作生成与未来世界预测相耦合。然而，这种假设的脆弱性尚未被充分研究。本文旨在揭示WAMs在面对对抗性攻击时的脆弱性，特别是当模型想象的未来与实际执行的动作之间出现不对齐时所带来的安全风险。

Method: 提出BadWAM框架来建模和评估世界-动作漂移攻击。该框架沿两个维度刻画攻击面：攻击强度和隐蔽性。设计了两种攻击类型：(1)动作优先攻击，直接驱动模型产生导致任务失败的动作；(2)想象保持攻击，在诱导有害动作转移的同时保持模型的预测未来接近正常想象。这些攻击使用小的视觉扰动来破坏WAMs中想象与执行之间的对齐。

Result: 实验表明BadWAM攻击显著降低了闭环执行下的任务成功率。动作优先攻击将模型性能从96.5%降至43.1%成功率。想象保持攻击进一步揭示了WAMs的特定漏洞：适度的未来保持正则化可以在维持强攻击性能的同时减少未来想象漂移，实现了隐蔽性更强的攻击。

Conclusion: WAMs耦合动作生成与未来世界预测的假设存在脆弱性。BadWAM成功展示了从显式动作劫持到隐蔽性攻击的失败谱系，揭示了WAMs在面对视觉扰动时想象与执行脱钩的严重安全隐患，这对WAMs的安全部署提出了重要警示。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) are emerging as a promising foundation for embodied control: rather than predicting actions alone, they learn representations that couple action generation with future world prediction. This coupling is often viewed as a source of robustness, interpretability, and safety, as a robot's action can in principle be checked against its imagined future. In this paper, we show that this assumption is fragile. We introduce BadWAM, a unified framework for modeling and evaluating World-Action Drift Attacks: a new class of WAM-specific adversarial attacks that use small visual perturbations to break the alignment between what a WAM imagines and what it executes. BadWAM characterizes this attack surface along two natural criteria: attack strength and stealthiness. When the adversary prioritizes disruption, BadWAM instantiates an action-only adversarial attack, which directly drives the model toward task-failing actions. When the adversary additionally prioritizes stealth, BadWAM instantiates an imagination-preserving adversarial attack, which seeks to induce harmful action shifts while keeping the model's predicted future close to its clean imagination. Together, these two attacks capture a spectrum of WAM-specific failures: from overt action hijacking to stealthier cases where the model appears to imagine a plausible future but executes a desynchronized action. We evaluate BadWAM across different variants of WAMs. Results show that our attacks substantially reduce task success rates under closed-loop execution. For example, our action-only attack reduces the model performance from 96.5% to 43.1% success. The results of our imagination-preserving attack further exposes a WAM-specific vulnerability: moderate future-preserving regularization can maintain strong attack performance while reducing future imagination drift.

</details>


### [84] [Data Driven Block Replacement Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15229)
*Aniruddhan Ganesaraman,VIdyadhar Kulkarni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对块更换策略提出了基于多臂老虎机的数据驱动算法，实现了O(K log T)的最优遗憾界，并通过嵌套观测特性进一步降低了遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于解决实际维护问题中的核心挑战：当设备寿命分布未知时，如何仅从运营数据中学习最优的块更换间隔k*，以实现成本最小化。传统的维护理论假设寿命分布已知，但现实中这一信息往往需要从包含右截尾数据的失败历史中估计。

Method: 本文采用多臂老虎机(MAB)框架对块更换决策问题进行建模，将不同的更换间隔k视为不同的臂。提出了基于Hoeffding和Bernstein不等式的置信下界算法(LCB)，利用块更换策略特有的嵌套观测性质设计了相关变体算法，并开发了基于Kaplan-Meier估计的非参数寿命分布估计方法。此外，构建了两种平均成本MDP模型进行理论分析。

Result: 理论结果表明：LCB算法达到O(K log T)的遗憾界，匹配Lai-Robbins下界；利用嵌套观测的相关算法实现O((K-k*)log T)的遗憾界，且对次优臂仅需O(1)次直接拉动；Kaplan-Meier算法实现了几乎必然的策略一致性。MDP分析证明了块更换策略在给定策略类中的最优性，以及在递增失效率分布下的单调阈值结构。

Conclusion: 本研究为未知寿命分布下的维护决策提供了理论完备且实际可行的数据驱动解决方案。算法不仅达到了理论最优性，数值实验还揭示了最优块更换与年龄依赖更换策略之间的结构性成本差距，为实际维护策略选择提供了重要指导。

Abstract: We develop data-driven algorithms for maintaining $N$ independent identical machines under a \textit{block replacement policy}, in which each machine is replaced upon failure and all machines are jointly replaced at regular intervals of length $k$. The goal is to learn the cost-minimizing interval $k^*$ from operational data when the lifetime distribution is unknown. At each decision epoch, the operator selects $k \in \{1, 2, \ldots, K\}$, observes the resulting failure history (a mixture of complete and right-censored lifetimes) and incurs a per-unit-time cost governed by the renewal function. We formulate this as a stochastic multi-armed bandit and propose Hoeffding- and Bernstein-based lower-confidence-bound algorithms achieving $O(K \log T)$ regret, matching the Lai--Robbins lower bound. Exploiting a nested observation property unique to block replacement, correlated variants attain $O((K-k^*)\log T)$ regret and require only $O(1)$ direct pulls of suboptimal arms $k < k^*$. A complementary Kaplan--Meier renewal algorithm estimates the lifetime distribution nonparametrically from censored data, achieving almost-sure policy consistency and empirically near-zero incremental regret at long horizons. We additionally analyze two average-cost MDPs: a time-elapsed formulation establishing that block replacement is optimal within its policy class for any lifetime distribution, and an age-vector formulation proving a monotone threshold structure under increasing failure rate distributions and providing a gold-standard cost benchmark. Numerical experiments confirm the theoretical ordering and reveal structural cost gaps between optimal block and age-dependent replacement.

</details>


### [85] [Mutable Low-Rank Sketches for Retrain-Free Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15242)
*Hector J. Garcia,Nick Clayton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出可变草图（mutable sketches）解决两阶段推荐系统中的嵌入陈旧问题，实现毫秒级在线更新且精度更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两阶段推荐系统存在嵌入陈旧瓶颈：用户评分新物品后，其嵌入向量保持不变直至下次重新训练，导致推荐滞后。

Method: 提出可变草图方法，使用KP树（带求和聚合的稀疏线段树）存储用户偏好，训练一次低秩投影，随评分到达实时重计算嵌入向量；采用范数比例采样策略处理稀疏数据。

Result: 在KuaiRec数据集上，可变草图以1.8%数据读取量达到0.810 RMSE（ALS需100%数据读取达0.822 RMSE），每批次更新速度快8倍；新用户首次评分后<1ms获得个性化推荐，无需模型重训练；范数比例采样在稀疏数据（<1%密度）上提供40-130%更好的物品覆盖率。

Conclusion: 可变草图有效解决嵌入陈旧问题，实现实时个性化推荐，且理论保证新观测单调收紧预测误差范围，在稀疏和稠密数据场景下均表现优异。

Abstract: A common bottleneck in two-stage recommendation is embedding staleness: when a user rates a new item, their embedding remains fixed until the next retrain cycle. We propose mutable sketches, which store each user's preferences in a KP-tree (a sparse segment tree with sum aggregation), fit a low-rank projection once, and recompute embeddings on-the-fly as ratings arrive. We prove that each new observation monotonically tightens the prediction error envelope (Theorem 1), a guarantee that FunkSVD and eALS lack. On KuaiRec, the mutable sketch achieves 0.810 RMSE at 1.8% data read vs. ALS 0.822 at 100%, with 8x faster per-batch updates. A new user receives personalized recommendations in <1 ms after their first rating, with no model retraining required. A comparison of sampling strategies across density regimes shows that the KP-tree's norm-proportional sampling provides 40-130% better item coverage on sparse data (<1% density), while uniform sampling suffices on dense matrices.

</details>


### [86] [Decoding Market Emotion from Blockchain Activity: A Data-Driven Sentiment Classifier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15258)
*Arthur G. Bubolz,Abreu Quevedo,Giancarlo Lucca,Rafael A. Berri,Eduardo Borges,Bruno L. Dalmazo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种结合链上数据、金融数据和社交媒体数据来解释比特币市场情绪的新方法，使用XGBoost模型实现了约0.84的F1分数，并通过SHAP方法提高了模型透明度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着比特币作为去中心化数字资产和投资工具的使用日益增长，理解其市场行为变得至关重要。现有研究多关注价格预测，而对市场情绪的解释性研究相对不足，缺乏将多源数据融合进行深入分析的框架。

Method: 研究融合了三类数据源：区块链交易数据（链上数据）、比特币历史价格数据和每日Twitter情绪分类。数据经过归一化处理后构建分析数据集。采用多种机器学习模型进行交叉验证测试，最终选择XGBoost作为最优模型。使用基于博弈论的SHAP方法量化链上特征对模型预测的贡献度，以提升模型的可解释性。

Result: XGBoost模型在情绪分类任务中表现最为稳定，平均F1分数约为0.84。数据融合方法产生了有意义的预测信号和洞察，SHAP分析揭示了链上特征对预测结果的具体贡献，验证了多源数据结合的有效性。

Conclusion: 研究证明了结合链上数据、金融数据和社交媒体数据可以有效分析比特币市场情绪，为数据驱动的加密货币分析提供了新范式。该方法具有良好的可扩展性，未来可通过深度学习技术进一步改进模型性能。

Abstract: The growing use of Bitcoin as a decentralized digital asset and investment tool has sparked strong interest in understanding its market behavior. This study presents a new approach to analyze Bitcoin market sentiment by combining on-chain and financial data with social media posts. Unlike models that aim to predict prices, this work focuses on explaining market sentiment using blockchain transactions, historical price data of Bitcoin, and daily Twitter sentiment classifications. The method merges sentiment trends with on-chain and financial metrics, normalized into a dataset for detailed market analysis. Multiple machine learning models were tested using cross-validation, with Gradient Boosting (XGBoost) emerging as the most reliable model for classifying sentiment, achieving an average F1-score of about 0.84. SHAP (SHapley Additive exPlanations), a game theory-based method for model interpretability, was used to quantify the contribution of on-chain features to the model's predictions, improving transparency. The results indicate that this data combination yields meaningful predictive signals and insights, supporting data-driven cryptocurrency analysis and future improvements with deep learning.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [87] [ExaGEMM: Exploration Framework for CPU-Driven ML Inference via Associative In-Register Computing for Low-Bit GEMM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14622)
*Hyunwoo Oh,Suyeon Jang,Hanning Chen,Sanggeon Yun,Ryozo Masukawa,Mohsen Imani*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出ExaGEMM框架，通过寄存器驻留LUT执行机制协同设计参数化内核与轻量级SIMD指令集扩展，实现CPU原生低位宽GEMM加速。在代表性ML模型上达到13.29倍延迟改善，有效解决了低位宽执行在传统CPU上的适配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低位宽GEMM对高效ML推理至关重要，但极低位宽执行在传统CPU上适配性差。实际部署面临碎片化精度区间（1/2/4位权重及不同激活精度），在固定SIMD和寄存器文件预算约束下，其可行性、复用机会和支持成本差异显著，使得轻量级CPU支持选择成为首要设计问题。

Method: 提出ExaGEMM工作负载感知协同设计框架，采用寄存器驻留LUT执行机制。核心洞察：现有SIMD数据通路已覆盖表生成和累加，仅需新增寄存器内选择/馈送机制。框架协同探索参数化内核与轻量级SIMD ISA支持，使用寄存器可行性、计算成本、内存流量和硬件开销的解析模型，在仿真前剪枝99.2%候选空间，识别非支配支持点并生成ISA规范、gem5补丁和GEMM内核进行验证。

Result: 在代表性ML模型和CPU目标上，相比纯软件基线实现13.29倍延迟改善。研究显示工作负载感知前沿选择对混合精度LLM工作负载尤为重要。

Conclusion: ExaGEMM证明了寄存器驻留LUT执行是CPU原生低位宽GEMM的有效实现路径，通过硬件-软件协同设计可显著提升性能。工作负载感知的前沿选择策略为混合精度LLM工作负载提供了关键指导，为未来CPU架构设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Low-bit GEMM is increasingly central to efficient ML inference, yet very-low-bit execution remains a poor fit for conventional CPUs. Practical deployment spans fragmented regimes-from 1/2/4-bit weights to varying activation precision-whose feasibility, reuse opportunity, and support cost differ under fixed SIMD and register-file budgets, making lightweight CPU support selection a first-class design problem. We present ExaGEMM, a workload-aware codesign and exploration framework for CPU-native low-bit GEMM via register-resident LUT execution. The key insight is that existing SIMD datapaths already cover table generation and accumulation; the only new hardware is an in-register select/feed mechanism with explicitly modeled cost. ExaGEMM co-explores parameterized kernels and lightweight SIMD ISA support using analytical models of register feasibility, compute cost, memory traffic, and hardware overhead, pruning the candidate space by 99.2% before simulation. It then identifies non-dominated support points and generates ISA specs, gem5 patches, and GEMM kernels for validation. Across representative ML models and CPU targets, ExaGEMM improves latency by 13.29x over software-only baselines, while showing that workload-aware frontier selection is especially important for mixed-precision LLM workloads.

</details>


### [88] [Toward Energy-Efficient and Low-Power Arrhythmia Detection for Wearable Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14747)
*Floriaan Bulten,Yawar Rasheed,Arlene John,Vincenzo Stoico,Ghayoor Gillani*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文通过采用数据精度降低和近似乘法等近似技术，在保持心律失常分类性能的前提下，显著降低了深度学习硬件架构的功耗和能耗，为可穿戴设备提供了更高效的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心血管疾病是全球主要死因，心律失常等疾病需要长期监测。现有可穿戴监测设备体积大、不舒适，且依赖医生手动评估心电图。深度学习算法虽在心律失常检测和分类方面表现出色，但其计算复杂度和高功耗限制了在可穿戴设备中的部署，亟需降低功耗的方法。

Method: 研究在先进深度学习模型及其硬件架构中应用近似技术，包括数据精度降低和近似乘法。利用MIT-BIH心律失常数据库对模型进行训练和验证，并综合评估采用不同近似乘法器的硬件实现方案。

Result: 与现有8.75 μW（2.08 μJ）的参考架构相比，所提架构在12 kHz时功耗为3.07 μW（2.17 μJ），功耗降低64.9%，分类准确率为93.7%，敏感性为92.1%。在100 MHz时，功耗为9.45 mW（0.8 μJ），能耗较现有架构降低61.5%。

Conclusion: 提出的近似技术显著延长了可穿戴设备的电池寿命，同时保持了所需的心律失常分类性能，证明了该方法在可穿戴健康监测设备中的有效性和实用价值。

Abstract: Cardiovascular diseases are the leading cause of death worldwide, and conditions such as arrhythmia often require long-term monitoring for effective detection and diagnosis. However, current wearable monitoring devices are bulky, uncomfortable, and typically rely on clinicians to manually evaluate electrocardiograms (ECGs). While Deep Learning (DL) algorithms have shown superior performance in arrhythmia detection and classification, their computational complexity coupled with high power consumption limit deployment in wearable devices. To address this challenge, this paper investigates the use of approximation techniques to reduce the power and energy consumption of DL architectures while maintaining acceptable classification performance. Specifically, techniques such as data precision reduction and approximate multiplication are investigated in a state-of-the-art DL model and its corresponding hardware architecture. The model is trained and validated using the MIT-BIH Arrhythmia Database, and hardware implementations employing various approximate multipliers are synthesized and evaluated. Compared with the state-of-the-art 8.75 μW (and 2.08 μJ) reference architecture, our proposed architecture consumes 3.07 μW (and 2.17 μJ) at 12 kHz, showing 64.9% reduction in power consumption while providing an acceptable output quality, i.e., 93.7% classification accuracy and 92.1% sensitivity. At 100 MHz, our proposed architecture consumes 9.45 mW (and 0.8 μJ), showing 61.5% reduction in energy consumption as compared to the state-of-the-art architecture. These results demonstrate that our proposed approximations significantly extend wearable device battery life while preserving the required arrhythmia classification performance.

</details>


### [89] [Valinor: Architectural Support for Fast, Energy-Efficient and Programmable Physical Memory Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14789)
*Konstantinos Kanellopoulos,Spiros Galanopoulos,Konstantinos Sgouras,Vlad-Petru Nitu,Ilias Papalamprou,Andreas Kosmas Kakolyris,Rahul Bera,Dimosthenis Masouros,Dimitrios Soudris,Onur Mutlu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出Valinor，一种硬件-操作系统协同的内存分配机制，通过可编程硬件分配引擎在接近纯硬件速度下执行操作系统提供的分配库，显著提升短生命周期工作负载的性能并降低能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统中，每次小页错误(minor page fault)都需要陷入内核，触发流水线刷新和停顿，经历长达数万周期的分配步骤。这些开销对于无服务器函数和微服务等短生命周期工作负载尤为显著，小页错误可占运行时间的54%和系统能耗的40%。先前的硬件分配方案虽然避免了陷入和上下文切换，但要么牺牲了有用的放置优化，要么依赖无法适应新策略或硬件条件变化的固定功能逻辑。

Method: 提出Valinor，一种硬件-操作系统协同的内存分配基础设施。Valinor引入可编程硬件分配引擎，以接近固定硬件的速度执行紧凑的操作系统提供的分配库。该方案支持多种策略，包括短生命周期对象分配器、完整性机制以及硬件遥测引导的内存放置。研究在运行Linux的BOOM RISC-V软核和全系统模拟器上实现了Valinor。

Result: 在实际硬件上，Valinor将分配加速17倍，端到端性能提升16%，能耗降低高达8%。全系统模拟进一步评估了可编程分配引擎和六个分配库，证明Valinor在提供硬件级性能的同时保持了可编程性。

Conclusion: Valinor成功结合了软件灵活性和硬件级性能，为内存分配问题提供了一个平衡的解决方案。通过可编程硬件分配引擎，系统可以在不牺牲可编程性的前提下获得接近固定硬件的分配性能，这对日益增长的短生命周期工作负载（如无服务器函数和微服务）具有重要意义。

Abstract: Physical memory allocation establishes virtual-to-physical mappings on demand. In current systems, each minor page fault traps into the kernel and triggers pipeline flushes, stalls, and a long sequence of allocation steps that can cost tens of thousands of cycles. These overheads are increasingly significant for short-lived workloads such as serverless functions and microservices, where minor faults can account for up to 54% of runtime and up to 40% of system energy. Prior hardware allocation proposals avoid traps and context switches, but either sacrifice useful placement optimizations or rely on fixed-function logic that cannot adapt to new policies or changing hardware conditions.
  We present Valinor, a hardware-OS cooperative memory allocation substrate that combines software flexibility with hardware-class performance. Valinor introduces a programmable hardware allocation engine that executes compact OS-supplied allocation libraries at close to fixed-hardware speed. It supports diverse policies, including short-lived object allocators, integrity mechanisms, and hardware-telemetry-guided placement. We implement Valinor on a BOOM RISC-V soft core running Linux and in a full-system simulator. On real hardware, Valinor accelerates allocation by 17x, improves end-to-end performance by 16%, and reduces energy consumption by up to 8%. Full-system simulation further evaluates the programmable allocation engine and six allocation libraries, showing that Valinor provides hardware-class performance without sacrificing programmability.

</details>


### [90] [CODA: Algorithm-Hardware Co-design for Edge Video Diffusion via NMP-Enabled Compute-Cache Operator Disaggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14908)
*Yuanpeng Zhang,YuXuan Wu,Yitong Xiao,Chenhao Xue,Yi Ren,Cong Li,Yihan Yin,Dimin Niu,Guangyu Sun*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出CODA架构，通过算法-硬件协同设计解决视频扩散模型在边缘设备上部署时跨时间步缓存面临的内存和通信瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散模型在边缘设备上部署具有重要价值，但基于Transformer的迭代去噪过程过慢。跨时间步缓存（CTC）虽能减少冗余计算，但在内存受限的边缘GPU上存在两大问题：（1）缓存占用大量内存，超出设备VRAM容量；（2）缓存操作与计算操作紧密交织，简单的近内存卸载仍会导致大量PCIe通信开销。

Method: 提出CODA架构，采用算法-硬件协同设计，核心思想是计算-缓存算子解耦。具体包括：（1）将密集计算路径和内存受限的缓存路径分别部署在xPU和轻量级DIMM侧近内存引擎上；（2）将碎片化的缓存活动重组为硬件友好的合并片段；（3）利用无分类器引导（CFG）分支独立性实现xPU计算与缓存侧执行的重叠。

Result: 实验表明，与现有最先进缓存算法相比，CODA实现了最高1.80倍端到端加速和1.74倍能效提升，同时保持了具有竞争力的生成质量。

Conclusion: CODA通过算子解耦和近内存处理有效解决了视频扩散模型在边缘设备上的部署瓶颈，为隐私保护的本地生成提供了可行的加速方案。

Abstract: Deploying Video Diffusion Models (VDMs) on edge devices is appealing for localized and privacy-preserving generation, but their iterative Transformer-based denoising remains too slow for practical local inference. Cross-Timestep Caching (CTC) has emerged as a promising direction for reducing redundant computation, reusing activations across adjacent denoising steps rather than modifying model weights, while largely preserving generation fidelity. However, on memory-constrained edge GPUs, CTC requires a massive cache footprint that quickly exceeds on-device VRAM and forces the cache into host memory. More fundamentally, cache operators remain tightly interleaved and chain-dependent with native compute operators, so naive near-memory offloading still incurs repeated PCIe exchanges for residual and fusion computations, turning cache reuse into a communication- and serialization-bound execution flow. We therefore propose CODA, an algorithm-hardware co-designed architecture centered on Compute-Cache Operator Disaggregation. CODA separates dense compute paths and memory-bound cache paths across the xPU and a lightweight DIMM-side near-memory engine, reorganizes fragmented cache activity into hardware-friendly coalesced segments, and exploits Classifier-Free Guidance (CFG) branch independence to overlap xPU compute with cache-side execution. Experiments show that CODA achieves up to 1.80x end-to-end speedup and 1.74x higher energy efficiency, while preserving competitive generation quality compared with a state-of-the-art caching algorithm.

</details>


### [91] [Differentiable Routability-Driven Package Floorplanning with Pin Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15005)
*Yiqi Huang,Zepeng Li,Zhen Zhuang,Kehao Chen,Genggeng Liu,Tsung-Yi Ho*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向先进封装的可微可布线性驱动布局与引脚分配算法，解决了扇出布线场景下的布线拥塞建模和引脚分配问题，在所有测试用例上实现了100%可布线性，线长最多减少约23%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着先进封装技术发展，重分布层(RDL)互连密度增加使可布线性成为封装布局的关键问题。功率完整性要求为电源分配网络(PDN)预留扇入区域，迫使信号网络通过扇出区域，加剧了布线拥塞。现有均匀网格拥塞模型无法准确表征扇出拥塞，传统引脚分配方法难以评估网络交叉问题。

Method: 提出三个核心方法：1) 可微线长最小化方法，直接建模离散芯片方向并通过反向传播线长梯度到芯片位置和方向；2) 基于多策略DPSO算法的交叉感知引脚分配方法，将网络交叉代价纳入优化目标，采用GPU并行代价评估；3) 可微可布线性最大化方法，构建针对扇出布线的拥塞模型，建立从拥塞信息到芯片位置的反向传播路径。

Result: 在所有基准测试上实现100%可布线性。对于基线方法成功布线的案例，与配备相同引脚分配流程的主流布局方法相比，线长最多减少约23%。

Conclusion: 该算法有效解决了先进封装扇出布线场景下的布局和引脚分配问题，通过可微分方法实现了端到端优化，显著提升了可布线性和线长优化效果，为先进封装设计提供了新的技术方案。

Abstract: As advanced packaging technology evolves, increasing interconnect density in redistribution layers (RDLs) makes routability critical to package floorplanning. Meanwhile, power integrity requirements often reserve fan-in regions for the power delivery network (PDN), forcing signal nets through fan-out regions and complicating routability estimation. Existing uniform grid-based congestion models cannot accurately characterize fan-out congestion, while previous pin assignment methods struggle to evaluate net crossings. We propose a differentiable routability-driven floorplanning and pin assignment algorithm for advanced packaging with fan-out routing. First, a differentiable wirelength minimization method directly models discrete chip orientations and back-propagates wirelength gradients to chip locations and orientations. It reduces wirelength under fixed pin selection while avoiding the bias of continuous-angle modeling. Second, a crossing-aware pin assignment method incorporates net-crossing cost into a multi-strategy DPSO algorithm and uses GPU-parallel cost evaluation to reduce wirelength efficiently. Finally, a differentiable routability maximization method constructs a congestion model tailored to fan-out routing and establishes a back-propagation path from congestion information to chip locations, thereby guiding routability optimization. Experimental results show that our method achieves 100% routability on all benchmarks. For cases successfully routed by the baselines, it reduces wirelength by up to approximately 23% compared with a leading floorplanning method equipped with our pin assignment flow.

</details>


### [92] [Pattern-Guided Design Space Exploration for FPGA Accelerator Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15068)
*Jialiang Zhang,Weiman Yan,Yuelin Zou*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出PATTERNDSE框架，通过识别计算模式（如逐元素映射、归约、矩阵运算等）来指导FPGA的高层综合设计空间探索，在保持最优性能的同时将搜索空间平均减少4.83倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然高层综合（HLS）将FPGA设计抽象至C/C++层面，但获得高质量结果仍需进行流水线、展开、分块、重排序和缓冲等调度决策，这会产生组合爆炸的设计空间；同时，许多数值计算核展现出可复用的计算模式，暗示着不同的优化策略，这启发了模式引导的DSE方法。

Method: 开发PATTERNDSE框架，基于Allo调度导向HLS编程系统，将包括逐元素映射、归约、矩阵-向量运算、矩阵-矩阵运算和模板类更新在内的常见计算模式映射到紧凑的调度空间；通过LLVM执行验证功能正确性，检查HLS C代码生成，使用轻量级模式感知评估器对候选方案排序，最后交由Vitis HLS综合。

Result: 在六个代表性核（vecadd、axpy、dot、matvec、gemm、jacobi2d）上评估，与exhaustive-lite基线相比，HLS评估候选数从140降至29，总体搜索减少4.83倍，单个核最高减少12.0倍；所有核均恢复与基线相同的最佳有效Vitis HLS延迟。

Conclusion: 计算模式信息能够有效剪枝低效的调度组合，显著减少设计空间探索开销，同时保持高质量HLS输出结果，验证了模式引导DSE方法的有效性。

Abstract: High-level synthesis (HLS) raises the abstraction level of FPGA accelerator design from hardware description languages to C/C++, but high-quality results still depend on schedule decisions such as pipelining, unrolling, tiling, reordering, and buffering. These decisions create a combinatorial design space, while many numerical kernels exhibit recurring computation patterns that suggest different optimization strategies. This paper presents PATTERNDSE, a lightweight pattern-guided design space exploration (DSE) framework for FPGA kernels written in Allo, a scheduling-oriented HLS programming system. PATTERNDSE maps recurring computation patterns, including elementwise maps, reductions, matrix-vector operations, matrix-matrix operations, and stencil-like updates, to compact schedule spaces. It then applies candidate schedules, validates functional correctness through LLVM execution, checks HLS C code generation, and uses a simple pattern-aware estimator to rank candidates before Vitis HLS synthesis. We evaluate PATTERNDSE on six representative kernels: vecadd, axpy, dot, matvec, gemm, and jacobi2d. Compared with an exhaustive-lite baseline, pattern-guided DSE reduces the number of HLS-evaluated candidates from 140 to 29, achieving a 4.83x overall search reduction and up to 12.0x reduction for individual kernels. Across all evaluated kernels, PATTERNDSE recovers the same best valid Vitis HLS latency as the exhaustive-lite baseline, demonstrating that computation-pattern information can prune unproductive schedule combinations while preserving high-quality HLS outcomes.

</details>


### [93] [NIFA: Nonlinear IMC enhanced FPGA for efficient ML inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15123)
*Jiajun Hu,Ruthwik Reddy Sunketa,Lei Zhao,Archit Gajjar,Luca Buonanno,Aman Arora*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型FPGA架构，集成无ADC的模拟内存计算（IMC）模块，使用ACAM替代传统ADC，原生支持非线性操作，并将IMC适用性扩展到Transformer的动态矩阵计算，在CNN和Transformer基准上分别实现40x和1.9x能效提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于ReRAM的IMC设计仅支持静态权重向量-矩阵乘法，Transformer模型中的非线性操作和动态矩阵乘法需回退到FPGA逻辑单元，导致增益受限；且ADC占据IMC模块70%以上的面积和功耗，严重限制系统效率与可扩展性。

Method: 提出ADC-free IMC模块，用模拟内容寻址存储器（ACAM）替代ADC，在模块内原生执行非线性操作；进行FPGA感知的设计空间探索以确定最优交叉阵列维度；开发高效映射策略，利用ACAM完成动态矩阵乘法，将IMC扩展至注意力计算。

Result: 在CNN和Transformer基准测试上，分别实现高达40x和1.9x的能效提升、4.1x和2.5x的面积效率提升；在长输入序列的Transformer工作负载上保持稳定增益。

Conclusion: 所提架构显著提升FPGA深度学习推理效率，突破了传统IMC在Transformer等动态模型上的局限性，推动了领域专用FPGA设计的发展。

Abstract: Recent FPGAs have improved deep learning (DL) inference efficiency through dedicated tensor blocks and in-BRAM computation. ReRAM-based analog in-memory computing (IMC) pushes efficiency further, offering an order-of-magnitude improvement in compute density and energy efficiency over conventional digital logic by performing vector-matrix multiplication (VMM) directly within the ReRAM crossbar; prior work has integrated such IMC blocks into FPGAs for DL inference. However, conventional IMC designs support only static-weight VMM, leaving nonlinear operations and dynamic matrix-matrix multiplication (DIMM) to the FPGA fabric. As a result, the benefits of IMC are largely confined to static-weight models, whereas Transformer-based models, which rely on frequent nonlinear and DIMM operations, gain only limited improvement. Moreover, the ADCs within each IMC block consume more than 70% of its area and power, further limiting system efficiency and scalability. To address these limitations, we propose a novel FPGA architecture that integrates an ADC-free IMC block, replacing the conventional ADC with analog content-addressable memories (ACAMs) that natively perform nonlinear operations inside the block. To fully exploit this block, we conduct an FPGA-aware design-space exploration that determines optimal crossbar dimensions while balancing FPGA area, flexibility, and DL performance, and we develop an efficient mapping that leverages ACAMs to carry out DIMM operations, extending the applicability of IMC to attention computation. On CNN and Transformer-based benchmarks, the proposed architecture achieves up to 40x and 1.9x higher energy efficiency and 4.1x and 2.5x higher area efficiency, respectively. Overall, it significantly improves FPGA DL inference efficiency and sustains robust gains on Transformer-based workloads across long input sequences, advancing domain-specialized FPGA design.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [94] [Intelligent Three Level Learning Architecture for Autonomous UAV Swarms in Search and Rescue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14093)
*Oleksii Bychkov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于搜救无人机群的三层分层学习架构，整合了赫布神经可塑性、多智能体强化学习与图神经网络、以及模型无关元学习等三种学习机制，通过22个架构契约和6个组件实现6类形式化保证，并引入群元认知概念以监控系统认知状态和切换策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分层强化学习方法存在根本性局限，传统方法通常在所有层级应用单一学习范式，无法充分利用不同层次（反射、技能、推理）的生物学层级特性。本研究旨在构建一种混合神经-符号系统架构，通过整合三种质上不同的学习机制来克服这些限制，并提供形式化的理论保证。

Method: 提出三层分层学习架构：(1)底层采用赫布神经可塑性实现个体智能体适应；(2)中间层使用图神经网络结合多智能体强化学习和行为树实现战术协调；(3)顶层利用模型无关元学习结合BDI推理和数字孪生进行战略决策。架构通过6个组件（BDI、行为树、GNN、MARL、神经可塑性、元学习）上的22个架构契约进行形式化，定义5个构造性进度函数连接抽象优化理论与具体操作场景，并引入群元认知作为三层结构交互的组合属性。

Result: 主要集成定理证明：当所有契约满足时，混合神经-符号系统可保持全部六类保证（安全性、预算正确性、最优性、活性、无饥饿、层级间一致性）。对于动态情形下的主动学习，新增5个契约扩展了三个额外保证（认知韧性、优雅降级、单调元改进）。理论分析表明该架构解决了现有分层强化学习方法的五个根本局限。

Conclusion: 该架构成功实现了生物学启发的三层学习机制的有机整合，通过严格的形式化框架提供了多维度的理论保证，为自主无人机群执行复杂搜救任务提供了可靠的理论基础，并在动态学习环境中实现了认知韧性和优雅降级等高级特性。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel three level hierarchical learning architecture for autonomous UAV swarms performing search and rescue operations. Unlike conventional approaches that apply a single learning paradigm across all hierarchy levels, the proposed architecture integrates three qualitatively different learning mechanisms corresponding to the biological hierarchy of reflexes, skills, and reasoning such as Hebbian neuroplasticity for individual agent adaptation, multi agent reinforcement learning with graph neural networks and behavior trees for tactical coordination, and model agnostic meta learning with BDI reasoning and a digital twin for strategic decision making.
  The architecture is formalized through twenty two architectural contracts organized across six components such as BDI, Behavior Trees, GNN, MARL, Neuroplasticity, Meta Learning that collectively provide six classes of formal guarantees such as safety, budget correctness, optimality, liveness, starvation freedom, and inter level consistency.
  We introduce Swarm Meta Cognition as a compositional property arising from the structured interaction of all three levels, enabling the swarm to monitor its own cognitive state and switch between cognitive strategies. Five constructive progress functions for SAR task types bridge the gap between abstract optimization theory and concrete operational scenarios.
  The main integration theorem establishes that when all contracts are satisfied, the hybrid neuro-symbolic system preserves all six guarantee classes. For the dynamic case with active learning, five new contracts extend the framework with three additional guarantees such as cognitive resilience, graceful degradation, and monotonic meta improvement. Theoretical analysis demonstrates that the architecture addresses five fundamental limitations of existing hierarchical RL approaches.

</details>


### [95] [HG-RAG: Hierarchy-Guided Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Structured Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14095)
*Pranav Yadav*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了HG-RAG框架，通过在层次化知识图上进行图遍历来为语言模型提供结构化上下文，在层次、关系和多跳推理任务上显著优于传统扁平检索基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RAG系统从扁平文档库检索上下文，在需要跨结构化知识的层次化或关系推理查询时表现不佳，无法有效处理层次性和关系性推理需求。

Method: 提出HG-RAG框架，在层次化知识图上进行图遍历。检索流程：从查询中解析命名实体锚点，然后向上通过父节点、横向通过关系邻居、向下通过子节点扩展上下文，实现多层次结构化信息检索。

Result: 在三个世界规模（18-800节点）和四种查询类型（局部事实、层次化、邻域、多跳）上评估，HG-RAG在层次化、关系和多跳推理任务上持续优于扁平检索基线，同时减少幻觉并保持局部一致性。

Conclusion: HG-RAG通过层次引导的图遍历检索有效提升了RAG系统在结构化推理任务上的性能，为需要层次化和关系推理的知识密集型应用提供了更优解决方案。

Abstract: Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG) has proven to be a widely successful process at improving the quality of outputs from a Large Language Model (LLM) for wider context. However, RAG systems typically retrieve context from flat document stores, which struggles when queries require hierarchical or relational reasoning across structured knowledge. I present HG-RAG (Hierarchy-Guided RAG), a framework that performs graph-traversal over a hierarchical knowledge graph to deliver structured context to a language model. My retrieval pipeline resolves a named entity anchor from the query, then expands context upward through parent nodes, laterally through relational neighbors, and downward through child nodes when needed. I evaluate HG-RAG against a dense retrieval baseline across three world scales (18-800 nodes) with four query types: local fact, hierarchical, neighborhood, and multi-hop. Results show HG-RAG consistently outperforms the flat baseline on hierarchical, relational, and multi-hop reasoning tasks, while reducing hallucination and maintaining locality coherence.

</details>


### [96] [IMEX Interaction-Based Model Explanation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14096)
*Emiliano Massi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为IMEX的模型解释框架，能够在黑盒预测模型中识别关键特征及其交互作用，并通过实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在预测建模中，黑盒模型缺乏透明性，难以解释和验证其预测决策的原因。在关键应用场景中，仅靠预测准确率是不够的，需要理解模型决策背后的机制和原因。

Method: 提出IMEX框架，基于两个互补指标：静态相关能力(PCS)量化单个特征的贡献，交互相关能力(PCI)捕捉特征间的非加性效应。该方法支持高阶交互分析，能够识别对目标预测贡献最大的变量及显著的变量交互，构建可解释性映射。

Result: 在三个具有已知结构的合成数据集上，通过INVASE方法的对比实验，验证了PCS组件的有效性。结果表明IMEX能够在输入特征与预测目标之间存在非线性、条件性和多重共线性关系的情况下，恢复相关的特征级结构。

Conclusion: IMEX框架成功实现了对预测模型的可解释性分析，能够有效识别特征重要性及交互模式，为黑盒模型的解释提供了新的方法论方向。

Abstract: In predictive modeling, the ability to explain why a model produces a given target prediction has become increasingly important [5, 10]. Black-box models do not provide a transparent description of the internal mechanisms that generate the prediction, making even accurate predictions difficult to interpret and validate. In critical contexts, predictive accuracy alone is not a sufficient validation metric if the reasons underlying model decisions remain unexplained. The IMEX (Interaction-Based Model Explanation) approach represents a methodological direction within explainable predictive modeling. IMEX is designed to identify which variables contribute most to the target prediction and which interactions among variables are significant in determining the target. The method does not impose limitations on higher-order interaction analysis, allowing the investigation of feature subsets with cardinality greater than two. Beyond the identification of feature importance, IMEX enables the exploration of interaction patterns that may be consistent with latent mechanisms influencing the outcome. Through the application of the IMEX algorithm, it is possible to construct an interpretability map of the predictions. The IMEX framework is built on two complementary metrics: Static Correlation Power (PCS), which quantifies the contribution of individual features, and Interaction Correlation Power (PCI), which captures non-additive effects among features. In the present work, the PCS component is experimentally validated through a comparison with INVASE [18] on three synthetic datasets with known structures. The results indicate that IMEX can recover relevant feature-level structures in the presence of non-linear, conditional, and multicollinear relationships between input features and prediction targets.

</details>


### [97] [RegNetAgents: A Multi-Agent Framework for Cross-Network Regulatory Driver Identification in Cancer Genomics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14097)
*Jose A. Bird*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了RegNetAgents，一个面向AI的多智能体框架，用于在异质性基因调控网络中进行结构化、查询驱动的调控候选基因识别，在乳腺癌和结直肠癌中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 癌症基因组学研究中，需要在异质性的基因调控网络（包括批量肿瘤数据和单细胞数据）中进行统一的候选调控基因识别。现有方法缺乏跨网络整合能力，且难以进行结构化的生物学解释和假设生成。

Method: 采用基于LangGraph的多智能体DAG工作流，整合TCGA来源的癌症网络和GREmLN项目的大规模单细胞调控网络。对给定靶基因执行双网络分类、基于OncoKB注释的癌症基因过滤、肿瘤来源调控关系的模式-of-action(MoA)分配，并按跨网络证据一致性对候选基因进行排序。系统提供统一的Python API和MCP客户端接口。

Result: 在11个乳腺癌和12个结直肠癌靶基因上的测试显示：TCGA来源的候选基因显示强富集（Stouffer Z=6.69和6.95），GREmLN来源候选基因也显著富集（Z=5.51和7.06，所有p<0.0001）。对照基因集（管家基因和非驱动基因）未显示富集，证明信号特异性。

Conclusion: RegNetAgents成功建立了一个可解释的AI框架，实现了跨网络调控候选基因识别，支持从候选识别到生物学假设生成的端到端解释，为癌症基因组学研究提供了新的分析工具。

Abstract: We introduce RegNetAgents, an AI-oriented multi-agent framework for structured, query-driven regulatory candidate identification across heterogeneous gene regulatory networks. The system enables unified analysis of bulk tumor and single-cell-derived ARACNe networks by integrating TCGA-derived cancer networks with large-scale single-cell regulatory networks from the GREmLN project. For a given focal gene, the framework performs dual-network classification, cancer gene filtering using OncoKB annotations, and mode-of-action (MoA) assignment for tumor-derived regulatory relationships. Candidates are ranked by evidence consistency across networks (Both, TCGA-only, GREmLN-only). The system is implemented as a multi-agent LangGraph DAG workflow, accessible through a unified Python API and Model Context Protocol (MCP) client, operating as a downstream analytical layer over precomputed regulatory networks rather than a network inference method. Across eleven breast cancer (BRCA) and twelve colorectal cancer (COAD) focal genes, RegNetAgents identifies candidate regulators significantly enriched for OncoKB-annotated cancer genes. TCGA-derived candidates show strong enrichment (Stouffer Z = 6.69 for BRCA and 6.95 for COAD), while GREmLN-derived candidates also demonstrate significant enrichment (Z = 5.51 for BRCA and 7.06 for COAD; all p < 0.0001). No enrichment is observed in housekeeping or non-driver control gene sets, supporting signal specificity. An extended module enables structured evaluation of oncogenic potential, druggability, clinical relevance, and network vulnerability, supporting end-to-end interpretation from candidate identification to biological hypothesis generation. RegNetAgents establishes an interpretable AI framework for cross-network regulatory candidate identification in cancer genomics.

</details>


### [98] [DialogueVPR: Towards Conversational Visual Place Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14115)
*Yukun Song,Changwei Wang,Xingtian Pei,Shibiao Xu,Wenhao Xu,Shunpeng Chen,Yu Zhang,Ke Zhang,Rongtao Xu,Xuxiang Feng,Pengyang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DlgPR任务，将地理定位转化为交互式对话推理过程，并构建首个大规模对话式基准数据集DlgQuest-Cities，通过DQ-pilot提问器与跨模态检索器的统一框架显著提升定位性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言引导的地理定位方法采用静态单次检索范式，无法有效处理真实自然语言描述中固有的歧义性和不完整性，缺乏人类交流空间信息时的交互推理能力。

Method: 提出DlgPR任务框架，构建DlgQuest-Cities基准数据集；设计统一推理框架，结合跨模态多层次检索器与智能提问器DQ-pilot；采用课程学习策略训练DQ-pilot：在DQ-cities-20k子集上监督微调，在DQ-cities-10k难例上通过GRPO进行强化优化；引入DDI指标指导课程采样和PRG奖励衡量问题带来的检索增益。

Result: 所提出的基于推理的方法在实验中显著优于基线方法，验证了对话式交互推理在地理定位任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: 将地理定位从静态检索转变为对话式推理检索是可行且有效的方向，DlgPR框架及DlgQuest-Cities数据集为该领域提供了新的研究基准，代码与模型已开源。

Abstract: Inspired by how humans communicate spatial information, language-guided geo-localization has gained significant traction for its intuitive and practical value. Despite this progress, most methods still rely on a static, one-shot retrieval paradigm, which fails to handle the ambiguity and incompleteness inherent in real-world natural language descriptions. We propose a paradigm shift to reasoning retrieval and introduce Dialogue Place Recognition (DlgPR), which casts localization as an interactive, dialogue-driven reasoning process. To support this new task, we present DlgQuest-Cities, the first large-scale dialogue-based benchmark for place recognition, and a unified reasoning framework that couples a cross-modal multi-level retriever with an intelligent questioner, DQ-pilot. DQ-pilot is trained in a curriculum: supervised fine-tuning on a curated DQ-cities-20k subset followed by reinforcement refinement on a harder DQ-cities-10k split via GRPO. Two task-aligned metrics guide learning: a Discriminative Difficulty Index (DDI) for curriculum sampling and a Positional Retrieval Gain (PRG) reward that directly measures retrieval improvement induced by a question. Experiments show this reasoning-based approach significantly outperforms baselines. The code and model are available at https://github.com/Graysonggg/DlgPR.

</details>


### [99] [Orchestrating Power Grid Studies with Multi-Agent AI and MCP Servers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14158)
*Jérôme Picault,Clément Goubet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了如何利用智能体AI和模型上下文协议(MCP)来支持输电系统运营商(TSO)环境下的电网研究，提出了基于MCP的pypowsybl-mcp接口以实现AI智能体与电力系统仿真工具的集成，为构建更具交互性、可审计性和可扩展性的电网研究环境奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 输电系统运营商在电网研究中面临仿真工具操作复杂、工作流程缺乏标准化、以及人工监督与自动化平衡的挑战。现有仿真工具难以被AI智能体直接调用，缺乏统一的工具调用接口，限制了智能体辅助电网研究的潜力。因此，本文旨在探索如何通过MCP协议整合大语言模型与数值仿真工具，实现结构化工作流程和人机协作，以满足工业界对智能体辅助电网研究的关键需求。

Method: 本文提出了pypowsybl-mcp，一个基于模型上下文协议(MCP)的接口，将电力系统仿真工具pypowsybl的能力暴露给AI智能体。该方法包括：（1）识别TSO环境下智能体辅助电网研究的工业需求；（2）设计标准化的工具调用接口，使智能体能够设置仿真、执行分析、检索结果并与仿真器交互；（3）提出人机协作、多智能体工作流的设计原则；（4）构建结合技术指标和实践者反馈的评估策略。

Result: 成功实现了pypowsybl-mcp接口作为测试平台，验证了AI智能体可以通过标准化工具调用完成仿真设置、分析执行、结果检索等任务。该接口为研究智能体与电力系统仿真器的交互提供了实验环境，证明了MCP协议在电网研究工具集成中的可行性。同时，建立了结合技术指标和实践者反馈的综合评估框架。

Conclusion: 基于MCP的工具集成是实现更具交互性、可审计性和可扩展性电网研究环境的重要一步。pypowsybl-mcp为智能体辅助电网研究提供了可行的技术路径，人机协作和多智能体工作流的设计原则为未来电力系统仿真自动化的发展奠定了基础，有望提升TSO的电网研究效率和可靠性。

Abstract: This position paper explores how Agentic AI and Model Context Protocol (MCP) can support power-grid studies in a Transmission System Operator (TSO) context. We focus on integrating Large Language Models with numerical simulation tools, structured workflows, and human supervision. We identify key industrial requirements for agent assisted grid studies and introduce pypowsybl-mcp, an MCP-based interface exposing selected capabilities of our simulation tool, pypowsybl to AI agents. This first step provides a testbed to study how agents can setup simulations, execute analyses, retrieve results, and interact with power-system simulators through standardized tool calls. We also discuss principles for human-in-the-loop, multi-agent workflows and outline an evaluation strategy combining technical metrics and practitioner feedback. The paper positions MCP-based tool integration as a step toward more interactive, auditable, and scalable grid-study environments.

</details>


### [100] [Interpretable Language Model for Closed-Loop Type 1 Diabetes Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14126)
*Maya Sarkar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LLM-T1D方法，将强化学习与大型语言模型结合，开发出既高效又可解释的胰岛素泵控制器，在FDA认证的模拟器上实现了优异的血糖控制效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于强化学习的人工胰腺系统虽然能够实现胰岛素输送自动化，但其"黑盒"特性导致医生和患者难以完全信任。作者希望开发一种既具有强化学习的精确性，又具有良好可解释性和透明度的胰岛素控制方案。

Method: 首先训练一个专家级强化学习系统，然后将其知识蒸馏到经过微调的LLaMA 3.1 8B和Qwen3 8B模型中。通过这种方式，将强化学习的决策能力转移到大型语言模型中，使控制器能够用自然语言解释其决策。系统在FDA批准的UVA/Padova T1D模拟器上进行测试，并对幻觉问题进行严格的形式化安全验证。

Result: LLM控制器在UVA/Padova T1D模拟器上表现出色，实现了73.5%的时间在目标范围内，超越了原始强化学习系统的性能。同时，控制器能够用通俗易懂的语言解释其决策过程，并通过了严格的形式化安全验证以防止模型产生幻觉。

Conclusion: 将大型语言模型与强化学习相结合的方法能够成功应用于1型糖尿病的胰岛素控制，不仅提高了控制性能，还解决了传统强化学习系统的可解释性和可信度问题，为医疗人工智能系统的透明化和可靠性提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Type 1 Diabetes (T1D) is a chronic, life-threatening autoimmune condition characterized by the complete destruction of insulin-producing pancreatic beta cells. While Artificial Pancreas Systems (APS) powered by Reinforcement Learning (RL) have shown promise in automating insulin delivery, their ``black-box'' nature makes it hard for patients and doctors to trust them fully. This paper presents LLM-T1D, a promising approach that combines the precision of RL with the clear, human-like reasoning of Large Language Models (LLMs) to create a more transparent and reliable insulin pump controller. By training an expert RL system and distilling its knowledge into fine-tuned LLaMA 3.1 8B and Qwen3 8B models, we developed a controller that not only surpasses the RL system's performance but also explains its decisions in plain, understandable language. Tested on the FDA-approved UVA/Padova T1D simulator, the LLM controllers deliver excellent blood sugar control (73.5% Time in Range) while maintaining strict formal safety verification against hallucinations.

</details>


### [101] [ReasFlow: Assisting Reasoning-Centric Scientific Discovery in Applied Mathematics via a Knowledge-Based Multi-Agent System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14178)
*Yutong He,Daibo Li,Guohong Li,Jiahe Geng,Zhengyang Huang,Can Ren,Zekun Zhang,Yifan Liu,Shuchen Zhu,Hengrui Zhang,Boao Kong,Ming Sun,Shu Li,Chenyi Li,Jiang Hu,Kun Yuan,Zaiwen Wen,Pingwen Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI
