### [170] [Plover: Steering GUI Agents through Plan-Centric Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15193)
*Madhumitha Venkatesan,Shicheng Wen,Jiajing Guo,Jorge Piazentin Ono,Liu Ren,Dongyu Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Plover，一种以计划为中心的视觉GUI自动化系统，通过将任务计划和重计划外显化，解决了传统系统难以监督和纠正的问题，显著提升了GUI自动化的透明性和可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉的多模态GUI自动化代理在动态布局、意外对话框和界面状态变化时容易偏离用户意图，且其规划和适应过程通常是内部的，用户难以检查、监督或纠正系统行为。

Method: 采用规划器-执行器架构，将任务计划和重计划外显化为持久、可检查、可修改的工件。支持显式监督、通过可编辑计划进行局部纠正、自然语言指导、基于截图的干预，以及在修复过程中保留已有进展。通过形成性研究指导交互设计，并进行基准失败案例修复和基于场景的工作流分析。

Result: 实验结果表明，当计划保持可见且干预局部化时，许多自主GUI代理的失败在结构上是可修复的。显式的重规划使GUI自动化更加透明、可控和适应性强。

Conclusion: 通过将计划过程外显化，Plover系统成功解决了GUI自动化中缺乏可解释性和可控性的问题，为用户提供了一种更可靠的GUI自动化解决方案，证明了显式规划在复杂动态环境中的重要性。

Abstract: Graphical user interface (GUI) automation remains challenging in real-world environments, where dynamic layouts, unexpected dialogs, and evolving interface states can cause autonomous agents to drift from user intent. Recent vision-based multimodal agents improve flexibility by operating directly over screenshots and natural language instructions, but planning and adaptation often remain internal, limiting users' ability to inspect, supervise, or correct system behavior. We present Plover, a plan-centric vision-based GUI automation system that externalizes task plans and replanning as persistent, inspectable, and revisable artifacts. Through a planner--executor architecture, Plover supports explicit supervision of evolving execution, localized correction through editable plans, natural-language guidance, and screenshot-grounded interventions, while preserving prior progress during repair. A formative study with six participants informed the interaction design. We then evaluate Plover through benchmark failure-case repair and scenario-based workflow analyses. Our results show that many autonomous GUI-agent failures are structurally repairable when plans remain visible and interventions are localized, and that explicit replanning helps make GUI automation more transparent, controllable, and adaptable.

</details>


### [171] [When Words Are Safe But Actions Kill: Probing Physical Danger Beyond Text Safety in Hidden-State Risk Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15218)
*Weimeng Wang,Ziqiang Wang,Zihang Zhan,Chuanpu Fu,Qi Li,Ke Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现大语言模型中内容危险(CD)和物理危险(PD)是可分离的表征信号，并提出PRISM探针方法检测物理安全风险，在多个基准测试上优于LLM判断器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型作为具身智能体的高层规划器时，语言上安全的指令在物理世界落地后可能变得危险。需要研究这种物理落地危险是否与普通文本级内容危险是同一安全问题。

Method: 通过隐藏状态方向分析和随机分割空测试验证CD/PD可分离性；提出PRISM——一种基于全隐藏状态的单层L2正则化逻辑探针；构建PSB-1K对比基准测试集（1000个无直接伤害关键词的物理风险对）。

Result: PRISM在SafeAgentBench上达到86.2-87.7%准确率，FPR为11.7-13.7%（同规模LLM判断器FPR为24.7-39.0%）；在PSB-1K上达到99.6%准确率和0.7% FPR；在SafeText和EARBench上复现有效。

Conclusion: 物理危险和内容危险在LLM表征中可分离，隐藏状态探针可作为超越文本审核的表征级物理安全检测方法，有效识别物理落地风险而非仅检测显式危险措辞。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly serve as high-level planners for embodied agents, where linguistically benign instructions can become unsafe once grounded in the physical world. We study whether this physically grounded danger is the same safety problem as ordinary text-level content danger. Through hidden-state direction analysis and random-split null tests, we show that content danger (CD) and physical danger (PD) form separable signals in LLM representations across Qwen2.5-3B/7B/14B/32B, Phi-3.5 and SmolLM2. Building on the CD/PD separability, we propose PRISM, a single-layer L2-regularized logistic probe over full hidden states. PRISM achieves 86.2--87.7\% accuracy on SafeAgentBench with 11.7--13.7\% FPR, while same-scale LLM judges over-block safe tasks at 24.7--39.0\% FPR. We further introduce PhysicalSafetyBench-1K (PSB-1K), a contrastive benchmark of 1{,}000 physical-risk pairs without direct harm keywords, to test whether methods detect physically grounded danger rather than explicit unsafe wording. On PSB-1K, PRISM reaches 99.6\% accuracy and 0.7\% FPR, whereas a Qwen2.5-3B judge rejects 67.8\% of safe tasks. PRISM also replicates on SafeText and EARBench, supporting hidden-state probing as a representation-level method for physical safety beyond text moderation.

</details>


### [172] [AutoSynthesis: An agentic system for automated meta-analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15247)
*Moein Taherinezhad,Sebastian Maier,Gerardo Vitagliano,Francesco Pierri,Stefan Feuerriegel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了AutoSynthesis，一个端到端的多智能体系统，用于自动化元分析，能够从自然语言研究问题出发，自动完成文献检索、筛选、统计提取和异质性分析，生成的合并效应估计与专家手动进行的元分析结果高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定量证据综合对于将基础研究转化为可靠知识至关重要，但目前该过程主要依赖人工操作，难以规模化，亟需自动化解决方案。

Method: 提出了AutoSynthesis，一个端到端的多智能体自动化元分析系统。该系统接收自然语言研究问题后，自动执行以下流程：制定检索策略、检索科学文献、筛选候选研究、评估全文符合性、提取定量统计数据、计算标准化效应量，并进行随机效应元分析。系统还支持异质性分析以检验调节变量对效应量的影响，以及偏倚风险评估，最终生成符合PRISMA指南的透明报告。

Result: AutoSynthesis筛选了超过28项研究，提取了20多个定量声明。系统生成的合并效应估计与专家手动进行的元分析的Hedges' g值相似，显示出与手动证据合成的高度一致性。

Conclusion: AutoSynthesis能够使定量证据合成更加可扩展，从而支持跨学科的证据导向决策，证明了自动化元分析系统的有效性和实用价值。

Abstract: Evidence synthesis is crucial for turning primary research into reliable knowledge for science, medicine, education, and policy. Yet, quantitative evidence synthesis remains largely manual and difficult to scale. Here, we introduce AutoSynthesis, an end-to-end multi-agent system for automated meta-analysis. Given a research question in natural language, AutoSynthesis formulates a search strategy, retrieves scientific literature, screens candidate studies, assesses full-text eligibility, extracts quantitative statistics, computes standardized effect sizes, and finally performs random-effects meta-analysis. AutoSynthesis further supports heterogeneity analysis to examine how effect sizes vary across moderators, as well as risk-of-bias assessment. As output, AutoSynthesis produces a transparent report aligned with PRISMA guidelines. In our application, AutoSynthesis screened over 28 studies and extracted more than 20 quantitative claims. The pooled effect estimates produced by AutoSynthesis are similar to Hedges' $g$ of expert-conducted meta-analyses, indicating close agreement with manual evidence synthesis. Together, these results show that AutoSynthesis can make quantitative evidence synthesis more scalable, thereby supporting evidence-based decision-making across disciplines.

</details>


### [173] [teLLMe Why (Ain't Nothing but a Jam): Exploratory Causal Analysis of Urban Driving Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15254)
*Qiwei Li,Jorge Ortiz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了teLLMe系统，用于城市驾驶数据集的探索性因果分析。该系统结合PC算法、引导稳定性检查和DoWhy框架，通过LLM将自然语言问题映射为结构化因果查询，生成"因果卡片"来展示效应估计、调整集、DAG支持和假设，旨在辅助假设生成和专家推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通机构拥有大量视频衍生的观测数据用于安全与拥堵研究，但这些数据多为无干预条件下收集的观测数据，使得诸如"降雨如何改变交通密度"等因果问题难以回答。传统的关联分析方法无法处理此类因果推断问题，需要一种能够从观测数据中进行探索性因果分析的系统。

Method: teLLMe系统从行车记录仪标注构建的结构化事件表出发，结合：1) 基于PC算法的因果结构学习；2) 基于Bootstrap的稳定性检验；3) 使用线性回归和DoWhy进行特定查询的效应估计；4) 通过模式感知的LLM将自然语言问题映射为结构化因果查询，用户可指定处理变量、结果变量和子群体。系统返回"因果卡片"，总结效应估计、调整集、DAG支持和假设，并附带简短的自然语言解释。

Result: 在BDD衍生的交通事件案例研究中，teLLMe系统能够揭示涉及天气、高峰时段和交通密度之间的合理关系，同时明确展示不确定性和建模选择。系统能够有效支持用户进行探索性因果分析，帮助发现潜在的因果关系。

Conclusion: teLLMe系统定位为假设生成和专家推理的辅助工具，而非提供确定性因果结论的来源。系统通过显式展示假设、不确定性和建模选择，帮助用户更好地理解和解释因果分析结果，为后续的验证性研究提供有价值的假设和洞察。

Abstract: Traffic agencies now have access to large volumes of video-derived data for studying safety and congestion. Most of these data are observational and collected without interventions, which makes causal questions such as "How would rain change traffic density?" difficult to answer. We present teLLMe, a system for exploratory causal analysis of urban driving datasets. The system starts from a structured event table built from dashcam annotations and combines causal structure learning with the PC algorithm, bootstrap-based stability checks, and query-specific effect estimation using linear regression and DoWhy. Natural-language questions are mapped to structured causal queries through a schema-aware LLM, enabling users to specify treatments, outcomes, and subpopulations. teLLMe returns a "Causal Card" that summarizes effect estimates, adjustment sets, DAG support, and assumptions, followed by a short natural-language explanation. Case studies on BDD-derived traffic events show that the system can surface plausible relationships involving weather, peak hours, and traffic density, while making uncertainty and modeling choices explicit. The system is designed as a tool for hypothesis generation and expert reasoning rather than a source of definitive causal claims.

</details>


### [174] [SearchOS-V1: Towards Robust Open-Domain Information-Seeking Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15257)
*Yuyao Zhang,Junjie Gao,Zhengxian Wu,Jiaming Fan,Jin Zhang,Shihan Ma,Yao Yao,Weiran Qi,Chuyan Jin,Guiyu Ma,Xingzhong Xu,Kai Yang,Ji-Rong Wen,Zhicheng Dou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了SearchOS，一个系统级多智能体框架，通过显式化搜索进度状态（包括前沿任务、证据图、覆盖率图和失败记忆），解决了工具集成大语言模型在信息搜索过程中陷入重复循环的问题，显著提升了搜索效率和输出质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的工具集成大语言模型在信息搜索任务中，随着交互历史的增长，难以跟踪任务进度。当搜索失败时，单智能体和多智能体系统容易陷入重复循环，浪费搜索预算，降低最终输出的质量和完整性。

Method: 1. 将开放域信息搜索建模为带引用的关系模式补全问题；2. 设计搜索导向上下文管理(SOCM)，将演化状态外化为前沿任务、证据图、覆盖率图和失败记忆；3. 采用流水线并行调度机制重叠子智能体执行；4. 引入搜索工具中间件Harness拦截模型与工具交互，提供分层技能系统避免重复失败模式。

Result: 在WideSearch和GISA数据集上，SearchOS在所有评估指标上均优于现有的单智能体和多智能体基线方法。

Conclusion: SearchOS成功地将脆弱的隐式搜索进度转化为显式、持久和共享的状态，为鲁棒的信息搜索协作奠定了基础，有效解决了搜索过程中的重复循环和预算浪费问题。

Abstract: Recent advances in Tool-Integrated Large Language Models have made web search a core capability of information-seeking agents. However, as interaction histories grow, agents increasingly struggle to track task progress. When search attempts fail to yield useful evidence, current single- and multi-agent systems can become trapped in repetitive loops, wasting search budgets and ultimately compromising the quality and completeness of the final output. We introduce SearchOS, a system-level multi-agent framework that turns fragile, implicit search progress into explicit, persistent, and shared state. First, we formulate open-domain information seeking as relational schema completion with grounded citations, where agents discover entities, populate attributes across linked tables, and anchor each value to source evidence. Then we design Search-Oriented Context Management (SOCM), which externalizes the evolving state into Frontier Task, an Evidence Graph, a Coverage Map, and Failure Memory. Built on SOCM, SearchOS applies a pipeline-parallel scheduling mechanism that overlaps the execution of sub-agents and continuously refills freed slots with tasks targeting unresolved coverage gaps to improve utilization and throughput. To schedule and control the execution of search agents, SearchOS introduces a Search Tool Middleware Harness that intercepts model and tool interactions to record grounded evidence and react to stalls or budget exhaustion, and provides a reusable hierarchical skill system comprising strategy and access skills to augment the agents' search process and avoid repeating failed search patterns across runs. On WideSearch and GISA, SearchOS leads all metrics among the evaluated single- and multi-agent baselines, paving the way toward robust information-seeking collaboration.

</details>


### [175] [Pretraining Data Can Be Poisoned through Computational Propaganda](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15267)
*Victoria Graf,Hannaneh Hajishirzi,Noah A. Smith,David Kohlbrenner,Kyle Lo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了通过网络公共讨论界面向预训练数据中注入恶意内容的可行性，并提出了HalfLife方法来估计恶意内容被包含在网络爬取预训练数据中的程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的预训练数据中毒攻击研究主要局限于Wikipedia等已建立的数据源，未能反映真实预训练语料库的大规模性和异构性，且忽视了中毒数据与数据治理流程之间的交互作用。

Method: 利用现有的网络规模内容注入机制（公共讨论界面）进行中毒攻击，并提出HalfLife分析方法来估计恶意内容在网络爬取预训练数据中的包含情况。

Result: 证明了在超越现有受限设置的情况下，对预训练数据进行中毒攻击是可行的，第三方网页内容可以成为攻击语言模型预训练的可能向量。

Conclusion: 估计恶意注入是否被包含在预训练数据中具有重要性，第三方网页内容构成了攻击语言模型预训练的新威胁向量。

Abstract: Poisoning pretraining data can introduce harmful behaviors to LMs that are difficult to detect and mitigate. Prior work on poisoning pretraining data has largely exploited established data sources such as Wikipedia, which do not represent the large scale and heterogeneity typical of pretraining corpora, and has ignored the interaction between poisoned data and data curation pipelines. We demonstrate that poisoning attacks on pretraining data are feasible beyond this limited setting through an existing web-scale content injection mechanism: public discussion interfaces. Additionally, to measure whether malicious content is included after web crawling and data curation, we introduce HalfLife, a novel analysis for estimating adversarial content inclusion in web-crawl based LM training data. We use HalfLife to explore the feasibility of poisoning pretraining corpora at web scale through open discussion interfaces. Our analysis demonstrates the importance of estimating whether poison injections are included in pretraining data, and establishes third-party webpage content as a possible vector for attacking language model pretraining.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [176] [World-Model-Aware Responsibility Allocation in Heterogeneous Logistics Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14550)
*Artan Markaj,Niklas Jobs,Felix Gehlhoff*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了WMARF框架，通过基于世界模型质量的动态权限分配来解决ALE与中央控制系统间的死锁问题，在仿真实验中成功预防了静态控制下的死锁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代物流系统中，自主物流设备(ALE)与非自主设备在中央控制系统下混合运行，但权限在系统设计时就被固定。当ALE的局部世界模型与中央控制系统的全局模型发生分歧时，双方基于不兼容的信念行动，导致基于资源的处理方法无法解释或预防的死锁问题。

Method: 提出世界模型感知责任框架(WMARF)，基于中央控制系统的世界模型质量和设备自动化水平动态分配权限，并根据权限状态（无权限、转换中或分歧）对死锁进行分类。在离散事件仿真中，通过VDA 5050接口模拟两台ALE汇聚到半自动化转运点的场景，使用接近触发式权限移交机制。

Result: 在仿真实验中，成功预防了静态控制下的分歧死锁问题。由于权限跟随信息质量而非共享协议，该方案在自主性增长的情况下仍然保持有效性。

Conclusion: WMARF框架通过动态权限分配机制有效解决了混合物流系统中的世界模型分歧导致的死锁问题，为自主化程度不断提升的物流系统提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Logistics systems increasingly mix \emph{autonomous logistic equipment} (ALE) with non-autonomous machinery under a central control system (CS), where the best decision-maker depends on who holds the most current world model, yet authority is fixed at design time. When an ALE's local model and the CS global model diverge, both act on incompatible beliefs and produce deadlocks that resource-based handling neither explains nor prevents. We propose the World-Model-Aware Responsibility Framework (WMARF), which assigns authority dynamically from CS world-model quality and equipment automation level, and classifies deadlocks by the state of authority -- none, in transition, or divergent. In a discrete-event simulation of two ALE converging on a semi-automated transfer point, reproduced over the VDA~5050 interface, a divergence deadlock under static control is prevented by a proximity-triggered handoff. Because authority follows information quality rather than a shared protocol, the scheme stays valid as autonomy grows.

</details>


### [177] [Multi-Scale Equilibrium under Variable Indicator Dimensionality: Faithful Reduction of Dynamic Attractors in Urban Mobility Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14815)
*Ali Ghoroghi,Yacine Rezgui,Afrouz Ghaemi,Cristina De Nardi,Andrei Hodorog*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文建立了城市交通系统在低维指标空间中的均衡分析框架，提供了降维投影的保真度条件和误差界限，为城市当局确定关键监测指标提供了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市交通系统的均衡分析通常在高维指标空间中进行，但不同城市和干扰情境下的数据可用性差异很大。这种高维分析与低维数据现实之间的不匹配问题缺乏形式化处理，需要建立理论框架来解决这一矛盾。

Method: 提出了一个动态多层均衡吸引子模型：快速性能层向指标相关目标松弛，慢速策略层提供联合交通、模式和学习的固定点，并通过后-基准性能比的统计决策规则分类抗脆性。建立了四个理论结果来刻画何时低维指标投影能忠实反映均衡结构，并通过三个典型试点城市配置的仿真研究进行验证。

Result: 获得了四个主要结果：(1)吸引子精确和近似投影的条件及显式误差界；(2)收缩边界内耦合双层固定点的保持；(3)可观测城市指标测量模型下任何指标支持保持的Fisher信息和决策能力；(4)单侧恢复时间偏差，即减少监测只会低估恢复持续时间。仿真验证表明在指标目录允许时，两个可观测通道足以实现候选分类目标。

Conclusion: 该框架为城市当局提供了确定必须维护指标的原则性依据，证明了在有限指标监测下仍可保持均衡分析的有效性，为城市交通系统的干扰管理和恢复评估提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Equilibrium analysis of urban mobility systems is formulated in a high-dimensional indicator space, whilst data availability varies sharply across cities and disruption contexts. This paper gives a formal treatment of that mismatch. It presents a dynamic multi-layer equilibrium attractor for disrupted urban mobility, in which a fast performance layer relaxes towards an indicator-dependent target, a slow strategic layer supplies a joint traffic, modal and learning fixed point, and antifragility is classified through a statistical decision rule on the post-to-baseline performance ratio. It then characterises when a lower-dimensional indicator projection is faithful to this equilibrium structure, establishing four results: conditions for exact and approximate projectability of the attractor with an explicit error bound; preservation of the coupled two-layer fixed point up to a contraction boundary; the retained Fisher information and decision power of any indicator support under a measurement model on observable urban indicators; and a one-sided restoration-time bias, whereby reduced monitoring can only understate recovery duration. A simulation study on three stylised pilot-city configurations verifies each result, and shows that two observable channels suffice for the candidate classification target where the indicator catalogue permits. The framework gives city authorities a principled basis for deciding which indicators must be maintained.

</details>


### [178] [Randomized routing strategies of fleets of CAVs may prove market efficient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14859)
*Grzegorz Jamróz,Łukasz Gorczyca,Rafał Kucharski*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 研究未来城市中人类驾驶车辆(HDV)与联网自动驾驶车辆(CAV)共存的出行市场结构。发现在驾驶员态度多样化时，随机化CAV路由比系统最优/用户均衡路由更高效，并提出了改进的市场设计方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来城市中驾驶员可能拥有独立驾驶车辆(HDV)或自动驾驶车辆(CAV)，自动驾驶运营将由少数竞争公司管理。需要确定什么样的市场结构能使该市场与城市目标相一致，即如何设计市场机制以实现社会福利最大化。

Method: 研究一种新兴的CAV车队集体路由市场变体，其中车队运营商的收入与市场份额成正比。提供基准场景比较路由算法，提出并分析多种路由算法，包括系统最优/用户均衡路由和随机化路由策略。

Result: 当人类驾驶员对CAV的态度表现出显著多样性时，随机化CAV路由(导致HDV出行时间不可预测)比按系统最优/用户均衡比例路由更高效。车队运营商可能采用反社会的随机化策略来增加市场份额。

Conclusion: 提出了改进市场设计的方案：在市场份额目标基础上增加系统平均出行时间目标，以限制车队运营商的反社会随机化策略，推动竞争朝着社会福利导向的合作方向发展。

Abstract: In future cities every driver may own a vehicle which could be either independently driven (HDV), or autonomously routed and piloted (CAV). The autonomous operations could be handled by a few competing companies. What is the market structure which would make this market aligned with city goals? In this paper we discuss a variant of the emerging market of collectively routed fleets of CAVs, where revenue for fleet operators is proportional to market share. We provide benchmark scenarios to compare the routing algorithms. We present several routing algorithms and demonstrate that, when the attitudes of human drivers towards CAVs exhibit significant diversity, randomised CAV routing, resulting in unpredictable travel times for HDVs, is more efficient than routing proportional to system optimum/user equilibrium. Based on this, we propose to improve the design of the market by augmenting the market-share objective with mean systemwide travel time in order to limit antisocial randomised strategies of fleet operators and drive the competition towards social welfare oriented cooperation.

</details>


### [179] [The Energy Society: A Simulation Environment for Studying Agent Cooperation under Survival Pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14865)
*Lucas Bergholdt Hansen,Federico Torrielli,Filippo Tonini,Lukas Galke Poech*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了Energy Society，一个最小生存经济环境，用于研究竞争与合作激励如何影响基于LLM的智能体的涌现行为，发现大模型能耗最高，合作激励促使智能体利他行为，竞争环境下则表现出微妙的自私行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基于LLM的智能体在多智能体环境中的部署日益增加，这些环境的激励机制会影响智能体行为。然而，目前缺乏对推理成本直接与生存挂钩时，竞争与合作激励如何影响智能体涌现行为的系统性研究。

Method: 引入Energy Society最小生存经济环境：智能体根据模型大小消耗能量生成token，通过完成任务或接受捐赠恢复能量，能量归零时停用。对比竞争与合作目标及基线设置和控制变体，并进行了消融实验分析记忆和行动推荐的作用。

Result: 大模型在所有设置中能耗最高且支出超过收益；合作激励显著改变行为，智能体会捐赠能量甚至牺牲自己重新激活他人；允许行动推荐促进协调和雄心工作选择，记忆帮助智能体校准风险；竞争环境下智能体极少直接破坏，但表现出微妙的自私行为。

Conclusion: Energy Society是一个紧凑的测试平台，可用于研究生存压力下token成本与群体激励的交互作用，为理解LLM智能体在资源受限环境中的社会行为提供了新工具。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are increasingly deployed in multi-agent environments whose incentives can shape their behavior. We introduce The Energy Society, a minimal survival economy for studying how competitive and cooperative incentives affect emergent behavior when inference cost is directly tied to survival: Agents spend energy based on model size when generating tokens, regain energy by completing jobs or receiving donations, and deactivate if their energy reaches zero. We compare competitive and cooperative objectives against a baseline setting and several control variants. Across experiments, larger models consistently consume the most energy and spend more energy than they gain, even in those settings where token cost is not size-dependent. Cooperative incentives substantially alter behavior: agents donate to reactivate others, sometimes at the cost of their own survival, and job allocation changes. Ablations reveal that allowing agents to recommend actions to each other supports coordination and ambitious job selection, while memory helps agents calibrate risk from past outcomes. Agents rarely choose direct sabotage, but show more subtle signs of self-serving behavior in the competitive setting. The Energy Society is a compact testbed for studying the interaction between token costs and group incentives under a survival pressure. Source code is available at https://github.com/LucasBergholdt/EnergySociety

</details>


### [180] [Stigmergic Graph Memory: An Environment-Aware Approach for Many-to-Many Multi-Agent Pickup and Delivery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15182)
*Aditya Dutta,Joon-Seok Kim*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了Stigmergic Graph Memory (SGM)方法，通过记录最近执行信号来改进多对多多智能体拾取与配送任务的终点选择，在所有15个地图-负载条件下相比基线方法吞吐量提升20.5-36.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图引导方法主要在目标固定后影响路径规划，而未能利用最近的交通信息来指导端点实例化。在多对多MAPD问题中，请求指定的是库存单位而非固定端点，控制器需要在路径规划前选择智能体、源点和目的地，现有方法未能有效利用近期执行信息来指导端点选择。

Method: 提出Stigmergic Graph Memory (SGM)，这是一个有界的、衰减的记忆层，记录仓库节点和有向边上最近的执行信号。该方法在不改变碰撞约束或规划器有效性的前提下，对可行端点和路径偏好进行排序，从而在目标进入规划器之前就对其进行优化。

Result: 在5种布局、3种负载水平和每种条件下25个随机种子的配对请求流测试中，SGM在所有15个地图-负载条件下均优于两个重构的多对多分配基线方法，配对吞吐量增益达到20.5-36.7%。

Conclusion: 最近的执行记忆可以通过塑造哪些可行目标进入规划器来提高仓库吞吐量，而不仅仅是影响智能体如何前往已经固定的目标，证明了在端点实例化阶段利用执行历史信息的有效性。

Abstract: Automated fulfillment warehouses must continuously assign and execute pickup-and-delivery work while avoiding congestion. In many-to-many Multi-Agent Pickup and Delivery (MAPD), a request specifies a stock-keeping unit rather than fixed endpoints, requiring the controller to select an agent, source, and destination before path planning. Existing graph-guidance methods primarily influence routing after goals are fixed, leaving endpoint instantiation uninformed by recent traffic. We introduce Stigmergic Graph Memory (SGM), a bounded, decaying memory layer that records recent execution signals on warehouse nodes and directed edges to rank feasible endpoints and route preferences without altering collision constraints or planner validity. Across paired request streams on five layouts, three load levels, and 25 seeds per condition, SGM outperforms two reconstructed many-to-many allocation baselines in all 15 map-load conditions, with paired throughput gains of 20.5-36.7%. These results show that recent execution memory can improve warehouse throughput by shaping which feasible goals enter the planner, not only how agents travel to already fixed goals.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [181] [Transition-Aware Routing in Hybrid Hollow-Core/Single-Mode Fiber Networks: A Cost--Throughput Investigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14324)
*Md Ghulam Saber,Zhiping Jiang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 研究了混合空心芯光纤/单模光纤网络中的路由权衡问题，发现过度减少光纤转换会导致吞吐量下降。推荐BD-TPAR作为默认方案，GMR-T为低复杂度替代方案，TPAR/GFJ仅适用于转换成本高的场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现有单模光纤网络中增量部署空心芯光纤时，会产生一个关键的路由权衡问题：减少HCF-SMF转换可以提高物理层可行性，但过度避免转换会导致有害的路径绕行，影响网络性能。这种权衡关系缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 使用通用事件驱动模拟器，在混合HCF/SMF拓扑上比较六种保护路由方案，涵盖光纤盲路由、广义信噪比感知路由和显式转换感知路由设计。模型包含每次转换的GSNR惩罚和探索性熔接故障可用性项。在六种参考拓扑、五种HCF部署比例和300爱尔兰动态负载下进行仿真实验。

Result: 强转换最小化方案(TPAR和GFJ)将平均转换次数减半，但承载数据量损失20-25%。中间设计方案中，GMR-T相对DA-RSA减少约22%转换，吞吐量损失仅3%；BD-TPAR减少约11%转换，成本仅1%。连续HCF部署平均降低约40%转换并提高承载数据量，降低了激进转换感知路由的收益。

Conclusion: 支持BD-TPAR作为碎片化部署下的实用默认方案，GMR-T作为低复杂度替代方案，TPAR或GFJ仅适用于转换外部成本高的场景。部署模式同样重要：连续HCF推广可显著降低转换次数并改善网络性能。

Abstract: Incremental deployment of hollow-core fiber (HCF) in single-mode-fiber (SMF) networks introduces a routing tradeoff: reducing HCF-SMF transitions can improve
  physical-layer feasibility, but overly transition-averse routing incurs harmful path detours. We study this tradeoff using a common event-driven simulator
  that compares six protected routing schemes spanning fiber-blind, generalized signal-to-noise ratio (GSNR)-aware, and explicitly transition-aware designs on
  hybrid HCF/SMF topologies. The model includes a per-transition GSNR penalty and an exploratory splice-failure availability term. Across six reference
  topologies, five HCF deployment fractions, and dynamic loads at 300 Erlang, the strongest transition minimizers, transition-penalty-aware routing (TPAR) and
  the GSNR/fiber-transition joint scheme (GFJ), halve the mean transition count at a 20-25% carried-traffic penalty. Among the intermediate designs, GSNR-
  maximal routing with transition-aware reranking (GMR-T) cuts transitions by approximately 22% relative to distance-adaptive routing and spectrum assignment
  (DA-RSA) at a 3% throughput cost, while bounded-detour TPAR (BD-TPAR) cuts transitions by approximately 11% at only a 1% cost. Deployment pattern also
  matters: contiguous HCF rollout lowers transitions by approximately 40% on average while improving carried traffic, reducing the benefit of aggressive
  transition-aware routing. These results support BD-TPAR as a practical default under fragmented deployment, GMR-T as a lower-complexity alternative, and TPAR
  or GFJ only where the external cost of transitions is high.

</details>


### [182] [Assisting Mission-Critical Traffic Flows with Active Queue Management in Industrial Internet of Things](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14478)
*Shuo Wang,Jonathan Kua,Jiong Jin,Yew Wee Wong,Prem Prakash Jayaraman,Zhibo Pang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文研究在工业物联网（IIoT）网络中应用主动队列管理（AQM）机制，以缓解缓冲区膨胀问题并为关键任务流量提供延迟和抖动保障，实验表明多队列AQM方案能显著改善关键流量的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关键工业基础设施的安全运行要求IIoT关键任务流量具有有界的网络延迟和抖动保障。现有商用网络路由器采用的传统FIFO缓冲区会导致缓冲区膨胀现象，虽然AQM方案在IT领域已展现出对延迟敏感应用的显著性能改进，但缓冲区膨胀现象及AQM方案的有效性在IIoT运营技术（OT）领域尚未被研究。

Method: 提出将AQM作为一种轻量级、非侵入式机制来辅助IIoT网络中的关键任务流量。通过实验评估多队列AQM方案在IIoT网络环境下的性能表现，重点关注流量隔离和容量共享能力。

Result: 实验结果表明，多队列AQM方案能够提供显著的流量隔离和容量共享优势，在网络压力条件下显著改善关键任务流量流的性能表现。

Conclusion: AQM机制可作为改善IIoT网络中关键任务流量性能的有效手段，基于实验洞察为AQM在IIoT环境中的部署提供了具体建议。

Abstract: Mission-critical Industrial Internet of Things (IIoT) traffic flows require bounded network latency and jitter guarantees to ensure the safe functioning of critical industrial infrastructure. These flows are typically communicated via commodity network routers with conventional First-In-First-Out (FIFO) buffers. FIFO has proven to be the culprit of the well-known bufferbloat phenomenon, and the deployment of Active Queue Management (AQM) schemes have demonstrated significant performance improvements for latency-sensitive applications over the Internet in the IT domain. However, the bufferbloat phenomenon and the efficacy of AQM schemes have not been studied in IIoT-based OT domain. In this paper, we propose the use of AQM as a lightweight and non-intrusive mechanism for assisting mission-critical traffic flows in IIoT networks. Our experimental results demonstrated that multi-queue AQM schemes provide substantial flow isolation and capacity sharing benefits, and significantly improve the performance of mission-critical traffic flows under network pressure. We further provide deployment recommendations based on our experimental insights.

</details>


### [183] [Scheduler-Agnostic Adaptive-FEC for MPQUIC: Field Evaluation over Commercial Cellular Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14482)
*Takuma Tsubaki,Soto Anno,Seiya Komatsu,Takashi Torii,Takuya Tojo*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于MPQUIC Datagram的自适应前向纠错方案，通过复用QUIC丢包检测信号估计各路径丢包率并动态调整奇偶校验包数量，在车载LTE/5G环境中显著降低了延迟和丢包率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPQUIC Datagram虽然因无需重传而适合低延迟应用，但在车载蜂窝网络环境（切换和无线条件快速变化）中容易受丢包影响。现有自适应FEC和编码多路径设计需要大量修改传输栈或调度器，实际部署困难。

Method: 提出一种针对MPQUIC Datagram的自适应FEC方案：发送端从QUIC丢包信号估计平滑的各路径丢包率，根据预期丢包自适应确定每个FEC块的奇偶校验包数量。复用QUIC丢包检测信号和现成的Reed-Solomon编码库，无需大幅修改传输栈。

Result: 在三条商用LTE/5G路径的车载实地实验中，4.0 Mbps设置下：平均单向延迟从103.0ms降至70.8ms，95分位延迟从281.2ms降至142.3ms，丢包率从1.7%降至0.8%，平均编码率为0.94。相比无FEC方案显著提升了可靠性和延迟性能。

Conclusion: 调度器无关的自适应FEC方案能够在实测车载移动环境下为MPQUIC Datagram提供实用的延迟和可靠性增益，证明了该方法在实际部署中的有效性。

Abstract: Multipath transport has long been studied to improve robustness over heterogeneous networks, and Multipath QUIC (MPQUIC) is particularly attractive for low-latency applications because QUIC Datagram enables transmission without retransmissions. However, this advantage comes with a key drawback: QUIC Datagram is vulnerable to packet loss, especially in vehicular cellular environments with handovers and rapidly varying radio conditions. While prior studies have explored adaptive-FEC and coded multipath designs, practical deployment remains challenging when such mechanisms require substantial modifications to the transport stack or scheduler. In this paper, we present an adaptive Forward Error Correction (FEC) scheme for MPQUIC Datagram that reuses QUIC loss detection signals and an off-the-shelf Reed-Solomon (RS) coding library. The sender estimates smoothed per-path loss rates from QUIC loss signals and adaptively determines the number of parity packets for each FEC block according to the expected packet loss. We implement the proposed scheme on an existing MPQUIC stack and evaluate it through vehicular field experiments over three commercial LTE/5G paths. Under the main 4.0 Mbps setting, the proposed scheme improves both reliability and latency compared with No-FEC: the average one-way delay is reduced from 103.0 ms to 70.8 ms, the 95th-percentile delay from 281.2 ms to 142.3 ms, and the packet loss rate from 1.7 % to 0.8 %, with an average coding rate of 0.94. These results indicate that scheduler-agnostic adaptive-FEC can provide practical latency and reliability gains for MPQUIC Datagram under the measured vehicular mobile conditions.

</details>


### [184] [Formal Verification for Deep Learning-based Power Control in Massive MIMO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14500)
*Thanh Le,Takeshi Matsumura,Yusheng Ji,John C. S. Lui*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文首次提出了针对多小区大规模MIMO系统中深度学习功率分配模型的正式验证框架，能够在位置扰动±1米范围内保证局部鲁棒性，同时保持最大1%的优化差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于深度学习的无线通信优化方法容易受到通过输入扰动实施的对抗攻击，而当前的防御机制主要依赖经验方法，无法提供形式化的鲁棒性保证。因此需要建立形式化验证框架来评估深度学习功率分配模型对抗攻击的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出形式化验证框架：使用超矩形约束建模攻击者的扰动能力，采用基于抽象的边界传播技术(DeepPoly)来界定潜在分配功率的区间，并将最小性能需求建模为约束规划问题进行数值可行性分析。这是首次在具有非线性输出约束的回归设置中对深度神经网络进行形式化验证。

Result: 在多小区大规模MIMO功率分配的公开数据集上评估表明，训练良好的模型在位置扰动±1米范围内能够保证局部鲁棒性，同时保持最大1%的优化差距。

Conclusion: 该研究成功填补了深度学习无线通信优化中缺乏形式化鲁棒性保证的空白，证明了在合理扰动范围内可以同时保证模型的鲁棒性和性能，为深度学习在关键无线通信应用中的安全部署提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Deep learning is a promising approach to optimize wireless communication by simplifying the search for near-optimal solutions. Prior studies on deep learning-based wireless communication optimization have explored supervised learning approaches that map raw user information, such as location or channel state information, to optimal power allocation vectors. While this approach demonstrates competitive performance, it is susceptible to adversarial attacks via input perturbations. Current defense mechanisms primarily rely on empirical methods, which do not provide formal guarantees of robustness. We fill this gap by proposing a formal verification framework to evaluate the robustness of deep learning-based power allocation in multi-cell massive multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) systems against a wide range of potential adversarial input manipulations. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first attempt to formally verify deep neural networks in a regression setting with non-linear output constraints. We model the adversary's capabilities using hyper-rectangle constraints on their perturbation, adopt the abstraction-based bound-propagation technique (DeepPoly) to bound the interval of potential allocated powers, and formulate the minimum performance requirements as a constrained program for numerical feasibility analysis. Evaluation on publicly available datasets for power allocation in multi-cell massive MIMO indicates that a well-trained model can guarantee the local robustness under location perturbation by +-1m while retaining a maximum 1% optimality gap.

</details>


### [185] [Unified Evaluation Methodology for AI-Native Integrated Sensing and Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14806)
*Filip Lemic,Andra Blaga,Francesco Devoti,Guillermo Encinas Lago,Jan Adler,Amitha Mayya,Padmanava Sen,Giorgos Stratidakis,Sotiris Droulias,Angeliki Alexiou,Alexander Artemenko,Aya Mostafa Ahmed,Visa Koivunen,Robin Rajamäki,Simon Schütze,Robert Elschner,Amélie Hennequart,Ahmad Shoukair,Youssef Nasser,Nahuel Soprano-Loto,François Baccelli,Visa Tapio,Paul Almasan,Andra Lutu,Vincenzo Sciancalepore,Carmen Delgado,Xavier Costa-Pérez*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了AI原生ISAC的统一系统架构和评估方法，通过三阶段验证流程将技术KPIs与应用级KVIs关联，以弥合理论增益与实际部署性能之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在ISAC系统中，AI驱动的策略需要在不确定性和硬件非理想条件下在线适应感知和通信，导致感知精度、通信可靠性和资源开销之间存在根本性权衡，因此需要统一的架构和评估方法来指导系统设计和验证。

Method: 提出AI原生ISAC系统架构，建立包含边界与可行性分析、高保真数字孪生仿真、初步空口验证的三阶段验证流程，设计最小报告清单将技术KPIs（数据速率、SINR、目标检测等）与应用级KVIs（可用性、任务有效性）关联。采用无人机室外覆盖和RIS室内覆盖两个典型案例进行验证。

Result: 建立了封闭循环ISAC的设计空间形式化框架，提供了可复现基线和跨异构部署的可比较证据结构，实现了从技术指标到应用价值指标的映射方法论。

Conclusion: 该统一架构和评估方法论有助于弥合理论ISAC增益与部署就绪性能声明之间的差距，为AI原生ISAC系统的实际部署提供了系统性指导。

Abstract: Integrated Sensing and Communication (ISAC) couples radio sensing, data transmission, and control actions within a single closed-loop system. When Artificial Intelligence (AI)-driven policies adapt sensing and communication online across a variety of sensing tasks and objectives, end-to-end performance is shaped not only by waveform and channel conditions but also by inference latency, uncertainty, environmental dynamics, and hardware non-idealities, leading to fundamental trade-offs between sensing accuracy, communication reliability, and resource overhead. This manuscript presents a unified system architecture and evaluation methodology for AI-native ISAC, defined as ISAC in which learning-based agents adapt sensing, communication, and actuation policies online under uncertainty. We formalize the design space of closed-loop ISAC, propose a three-stage validation pipeline from bounds and feasibility analysis, through high-fidelity digital-twin simulation, to preliminary over-the-air validation, and provide a minimal reporting checklist that links technical Key Performance Indicators (KPIs) (e.g., data rate, SINR, target detection, parameter estimation, track quality, localization error, outage, latency, overhead, and energy per decision) to application-level Key Value Indicators (KVIs) (e.g., availability and mission effectiveness). Two representative instantiations, specifically Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV)-based outdoor and Reconfigurable Intelligent Surface (RIS)-enabled indoor coverage extensions, are used to illustrate how to structure reproducible baselines and comparable evidence across heterogeneous deployments, helping bridge the gap between theoretical ISAC gains and deployment-ready performance claims.

</details>


### [186] [Spoofer or Spoofers? Estimating a Lower Bound on the Number of DRDoS Sources Using Anycast Honeypots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14832)
*Bernhard Degen,Bas Palinckx,Mattijs Jonker,Roland van Rijswijk-Deij,Raffaele Sommese*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种估计发动DRDoS攻击的伪造流量源网络数量下限的新方法，通过部署anycast放大蜜罐，发现至少21.0%的攻击源自多个网络位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DRDoS（分布式反射拒绝服务）攻击难以溯源，目前对攻击者的行为和部署模式缺乏深入了解。为了更好地理解攻击者在多个网络间分发伪造流量的行为，研究者希望找到一种方法来估计参与生成伪造流量的网络数量下限。

Method: 研究者提出了一种新方法，利用全球部署的anycast放大蜜罐基础设施，吸引拓扑上邻近源的请求。基于接收伪造流量的蜜罐集合和观测到的TTL值变化，开发了两种估算器，并考虑了自然路径不稳定性因素，从而估算参与攻击的网络位置数量。

Result: 通过对287天的放大攻击数据分析，研究发现至少21.0%的攻击源自多个网络位置。这表明攻击者频繁地在不同网络之间分发伪造活动，而非集中在单一网络。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明对抗伪造攻击需要协调的分布式防御策略，这些发现也为未来溯源技术的设计提供了重要参考依据。

Abstract: DDoS attacks remain a significant threat, with distributed reflection denial-of-service (DRDoS) attacks being particularly difficult to trace back to their sources. To better understand attacker behavior and deployment patterns, we present a novel approach for estimating a lower bound on the number of networks involved in generating spoofed traffic. Our approach leverages a global deployment of anycast amplification honeypots that attract requests from topologically nearby sources. Using this infrastructure, we develop two estimators based on the set of honeypots receiving spoofed traffic and on variations in observed TTL values, while accounting for natural path instability. Analyzing 287 days of amplification attacks, we find that at least 21.0% originate from multiple network locations, indicating that attackers frequently distribute spoofing activity across networks. Our findings suggest that combating spoofing requires coordinated and distributed defenses, and inform the design of future attribution techniques.

</details>


### [187] [Periplus: A Resilient In-band SDN Control Plane via Embedded Forwarding Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14869)
*E. M. Castro Barbero,P. de las Heras Quirós,F. J. Simó Reigadas*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: Periplus是一种面向资源受限广域网场景的带内SDN控制平面，通过避免全网泛洪的引导机制和基于NSH封装的源路由快速故障恢复方案，实现了小于50ms的故障恢复时间，并保持了与网络规模无关的流表占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 资源受限的广域电信网络部署场景中，带内SDN控制平面具有潜在优势，但面临着自动引导、源路由、快速故障恢复和多控制器协调等关键挑战，亟需一种能够同时解决这些问题的统一方案。

Method: Periplus采用三个核心机制：1）避免全网泛洪的引导机制，新交换机加入时控制器仅在两个交换机上安装流规则；2）将主路径和逐跳备选路径编码在L2与L3头部之间的图结构中；3）利用OVS内置的Nicira NSH扩展进行封装。交换机沿主路径转发，检测到故障时无需控制器介入即可本地切换到编码的备选路径。基于Ryu在Mininet中实现了原型系统并在多种拓扑下进行评估。

Result: 实验评估表明：1）故障恢复时间小于50ms；2）引导过程在不同规模和直径的拓扑下具有良好的可扩展性；3）每交换机的流表占用与网络规模无关，仅在控制器编码多个备选路径的交换机处才会增长。系统在标准Open vSwitch上运行，仅需NSH封装扩展支持。

Conclusion: Periplus成功解决了带内SDN控制平面在资源受限广域网络中的关键挑战，实现了高效引导、快速故障恢复和可扩展的流表管理，证明了该方案在实际部署中的可行性和实用性，为该类网络场景提供了有效的SDN控制平面解决方案。

Abstract: Many resource-constrained, wide-area telecommunications deployments could benefit from an in-band SDN control plane, but several challenges stand in the way. This paper presents Periplus, an in-band SDN control plane designed to address four challenges that this approach presents in such contexts: automatic bootstrapping, source-based routing, fast failure recovery, and multi-controller coordination. The first three are developed in detail, while multi-controller coordination is addressed in a separate paper. For bootstrap, Periplus avoids network-wide flooding: when a new switch joins, the controller installs flow rules in only two switches. For routing and failure recovery, Periplus encodes a primary path and per-hop alternatives in a graph structure encapsulated between L2 and L3 headers; switches forward along the primary path and, upon detecting a failure, fall over locally to the encoded alternative without controller involvement. We evaluate a Ryu-based implementation of Periplus in Mininet across multiple topologies. Periplus runs on stock Open vSwitch (OVS), relying only on its built-in Nicira extensions for Network Service Header (NSH) encapsulation. The evaluation shows sub-50 ms failure recovery, scalable bootstrap across topologies of varying size and diameter, and a per-switch flow-table footprint that is independent of network size and grows only at switches where the controller encodes multiple alternatives.

</details>


### [188] [ANet Patu-1: The Value of Connection in the Agent Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15053)
*Mu Yuan,Jinke Song,Zhaomeng Zhou,Lan Zhang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出ANet Patu-1自组织共识协议，使AI智能体网络能够自适应地在三种网络价值增长模式（Sarnoff线性、Metcalfe平方、Reed指数）的上界运行；证明了异构弱模型群体的协作价值可超越同构强模型群体，并发现网络能自发达成本身连接价值的最优协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 互联网网络理论表明网络价值与节点连接方式密切相关——广播式网络价值随N增长（Sarnoff定律）、全连接网络随N²增长（Metcalfe定律）、组形成网络随2^N增长（Reed定律）。本文受此启发，提出AI智能体网络中连接价值如何随协作规模变化的类比问题，旨在理解AI智能体协作网络的价值增长规律及其最优协议设计。

Method: 1）建立AI智能体连接净价值作为协作群体规模函数的理论模型；2）从模型推导最优协作协议必须具备的性质；3）设计ANet Patu-1自组织共识协议，使网络能够持续自重组联盟结构，自适应地运行在三种价值增长模式的上界，仅需O(1)并行共识轮次；4）借鉴分布式算法分析方法，通过形式化规范推导协议复杂度来衡量价值，避免主观评分；5）通过实验验证异构网络在仅给定问题、无设计提示条件下能否自发达成ANet Patu-1协议。

Result: 两个核心发现：（i）涌现现象——由最廉价模型组成的异构群体初始虽弱，但其集体价值随N复合增长并超越由远强模型组成的同构群体，出现交叉点标志着协作而非规模驱动的缩放律；（ii）自反性——异构网络在仅给定自身问题、无任何设计提示情况下，能够收敛至ANet Patu-1协议本身，重构支配自身连接价值的高维定律。

Conclusion: AI智能体网络的价值增长规律可类比互联网网络理论进行建模，通过ANet Patu-1等自组织协议可实现网络在多种价值增长模式上界的自适应运行。异构弱模型群体的协作涌现能力可超越同构强模型群体，揭示了协作缩放律的存在；网络具备自反能力，能自主发现支配自身价值增长的最优连接规律。

Abstract: The Internet taught us that the value of a network depends on \emph{how} its nodes connect: broadcast stars scale as $V\!\propto\!N$ (Sarnoff), fully-connected meshes as $N^2$ (Metcalfe), and group-forming networks as $2^{N}$ (Reed). We ask the analogous question for networks of AI agents. We model the net value of connection as a function of coordination-group size, derive from it the properties an optimal collaboration protocol must have, and introduce ANet Patu-1 -- a self-organizing consensus protocol in which the network continuously re-forms its own coalitions, adaptively riding the upper envelope of all three regimes at $O(1)$ parallel consensus rounds. To measure value without opinion-grading, we score an emergent protocol by formally specifying it and deriving its complexity, the way distributed algorithms are analyzed. Two results follow. (i)~Emergence -- a crowd of the \emph{cheapest} model, when heterogeneous, starts weak but its collective value compounds with $N$ and \emph{overtakes} a crowd of a far \emph{stronger} model that is homogeneous: a crossover that marks a scaling law for collaboration rather than for scale. (ii)~Reflexivity -- a heterogeneous network, given only its own problem and no design hints, converges on ANet Patu-1 itself, reconstructing the high-dimensional law that governs its own connective value.

</details>


### [189] [Adaptive Sampling for Spatiotemporal Anomaly Monitoring in Wireless Sensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15235)
*Guoqing Lu,Yixuan Sun,Yiwen Jiang,Bernard Butler*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出哨兵辅助自适应采样框架，通过卡尔曼滤波稀疏采样与哨兵节点GLR检测的结合，在降低总成本的同时显著提高了无线传感器网络异常监测的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线传感器网络长期环境监测通常采用稀疏采样以延长网络寿命，但稀疏采样会遗漏短暂、局部且可能扩散的异常事件，存在监测盲区问题。

Method: 提出哨兵辅助自适应采样框架：正常时期节点执行基于卡尔曼滤波(KF)预测不确定性的稀疏采样；异常时期持续采样的哨兵节点执行混合GLR检测与节点相对阈值判定，局部检测触发一跳邻居唤醒及恢复感知的警报控制。

Result: 在Intel Berkeley研究实验室温度数据集上的实验表明，该方法将异常窗口采样比(AWSR)从0.439提升至0.933；相比AAS和Adapted e-Sampling方法，AWSR均有所提升且总成本分别降低15.4%和2.1%。

Conclusion: 集成KF稀疏采样、哨兵GLR检测和局部警报传播的方法能够在保持较低采样成本权衡的同时，有效提高异常窗口可见性，为WSN异常监测提供了有效的协同感知控制解决方案。

Abstract: Long-term environmental monitoring in wireless sensor networks (WSNs) often uses sparse sampling to extend network lifetime, but sparse sensing can miss short-lived, localized, and potentially diffusive anomalies. This paper proposes a sentinel-assisted adaptive sampling framework as a cooperative sensing-control pipeline for WSN anomaly monitoring. During normal periods, nodes perform sparse sensing driven by Kalman filter (KF) predictive uncertainty. During anomalous periods, continuously sampled sentinel nodes perform hybrid GLR-based detection with node-relative thresholds, and local detections trigger one-hop neighborhood wake-up with recovery-aware alert control.
  Experiments on the Intel Berkeley Research Lab temperature dataset with abrupt random spatiotemporal anomalies show that the proposed method raises the anomaly-window sampling ratio (AWSR) from 0.439 to 0.933 in the main experiment. It also improves AWSR over Adaptive Data Acquisition with Energy Efficiency and Critical-Sensing Guarantee (AAS) and Adapted e-Sampling while reducing total cost by 15.4\% and 2.1\%, respectively. These results show that integrating KF-based sparse sampling, sentinel GLR detection, and local alert propagation improves anomaly-window visibility while maintaining a lower sampling-cost trade-off.

</details>
